## Honolulu

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmi states in *The Nectar of Devotion,* “Always remember Kṛṣṇa, never forget Him.” This is the sum and substance of your teachings: to be conscious of Kṛṣṇa twenty-four hours a day. In fact, it is Kṛṣṇa Himself as the Supersoul who gives us remembrance or forgetfulness of Him. As you state so nicely in one of your purports to the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.6.9):

“The conditioned state is due to the soul’s forgetfulness of his relationship of subordination to the Lord. One who wants to continue to forget the Lord is helped by the Lord to forget Him birth after birth. But one who remembers Him by dint of association with a devotee of the Lord is helped to remember Him more and more. Thus, a conditioned soul can ultimately go back home, back to Godhead.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you bring about this remembrance of Kṛṣṇa because you are always thinking of Kṛṣṇa. You are the touchstone that makes a people Kṛṣṇa conscious. You are the boat that carries us across the ocean of this material existence. You are the catalyst that will keep this movement united. And you are the source of strength that will keep this movement pure.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you said that the debt to the spiritual master can never be repaid, but that we could attempt to pay it by preaching vigorously as you have done. So we hope to continue this mood of preaching, which you showed us on so many occasions.

Here is an excerpt from a room conversation in Honolulu, Hawaii, on May 7, 1976:

**Prabhupāda**: So in this way…… You are all intelligent, you can plan. The aim is how to distribute books. That is the first consideration. In *Bhāgavata* it is very figuratively described that we have got this body and the different parts. Just like Arjuna is sitting on the chariot. There is chariot driver, there are horses, reins. There is field, and the arrow, and the bow. They have been figuratively. So this can be used for killing the enemies of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness and then give up all this paraphernalia, chariot, we…..Just like after fighting, only victory, then you kill them. And similarly this body is there, the mind is there, the senses are there. So utilize it for conquering over this material existence. And then give up this body and go back to home.  
**Tamāl Krishna:** Does the devotee, I mean just like you are always enthusing us to push on….  
**Prabhupāda**: That is sharpening your weapons. That is also described. By serving the spiritual master, you keep your weapon always sharpened. And them take help from Kṛṣṇa, the words of spiritual master sharpen weapon and *yasya prasādād bhagavat….*and the spiritual master is happy, then Kṛṣṇa immediately will help. He gives you strength. Suppose you have a sword? Kṛṣṇa will give you strength how to fight and kill the enemies. Everything is described. Therefore Caitanya Mahāprabhu said *guru-Kṛṣṇa-kṛpāya,* make your weapon sharpened by the instruction of the spiritual master and then Kṛṣṇa will give you the strength, you’ll be able to conquer.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing us to take part in the battle against *māyā.* It is not always blissful. There is some anxiety in pushing on this movement. But to take on a little anxiety for you is the only way in which we can try to repay you for all the wonderful gifts you have given us.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

(written by Rādhānātha dāsa)

*Your servant at the Honolulu temple.*

## Houston

*Śrīla Prabhupāda, I saw your disciples,*  
*And they were not composing poetry of prose.*  
*Although the Vyāsa-pūjā deadline fast approaches,*  
*So does the threat that your temples will close.*  
*I saw them after midnight,*  
*When devotees should be asleep.*  
*Instead they were congregating*  
*In the darkness of the street.*  
*I saw them on Sundays,*  
*When devotees should take the feast.*  
*Instead they were fasting,*  
*Until the persecution’s ceased.*  
*I saw them disguised in suits and ties,*  
*Carrying lawbooks,*  
*I saw them in shaven heads,*  
*Tolerating dirty looks.*  
*I saw them in the offices,*  
*Late into the night,*  
*And first thing in the morning,*  
*Keeping up the fight.*  
*I saw them giving time and money,*  
*Putting aside their personal lives——-*  
*Brahmacārīs, sannyāsīs,*  
*Husbands and their wives.*  
*I saw them in anxiety,*  
*Tears were in their eyes,*  
*Like children facing a beast*  
*Of gigantic size.*  
*I saw them full of courage,*  
*Clinging to your feet,*  
*Praying for your guidance*  
*The way a kīrtan needs a beat.*  
*We are all your instruments,*  
*Waiting to be engaged.*  
*Your instructions are our melody,*  
*But the demons are enraged.*  
*Prabhupāda, I saw your devotees*  
*Cooperating for your sake.*  
*That was more of an offering*  
*Than any poem I could make.*

(written by Gopālācārya dāsa)

*Your devotees and followers in Houston, Texas.*

## Isreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this, your auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, we would simply like to thank you for giving us the opportunity to perform devotional service in this part of the world. Each year the people of Israel, both Jew and Arab, are showing increased support for Lord Caitanya ‘s *saṅkīrtana* movement, demonstrating clearly that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the only peace formula. By your mercy, more of your transcendental books were distributed to the local population in the previous year than ever in the past. As this area of the planet is currently known primarily conflict and noncooperation, we ask your blessings so that we, your unqualified servants in Isreal, can set a very nice example for the world by working together cooperatively to spread the glories of Kṛṣṇa and Your Divine Grace.

All glories to Śrī Kṛṣṇa Saṅkīrtana ! All Glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servant in the Israel yātrā.*

## Italy (northwest)

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine appearance day.

Generally we go on performing our service without being sufficiently aware of the cause of that service. We take it for granted that the service is there. It comes to us automatically, and we are engaged as a matter of course, mainly due to our willingness to go along with the process.

Philosophically we understand that service comes from Kṛṣṇa. It is nondifferent from Him, and you are delivering it to us. But in the ordinary performance of our activities we often forget your continuous support. We may even think that devotional service depends on us and that we are in control of the situation.

Without the mercy of the spiritual master, one cannot even chant Hare Kṛṣṇa once. Kṛṣṇa is the Soul of our soul, and you are the devotion of our devotion. That means you are the origin of our desire to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. You guide our intelligence on the path of progress in spiritual life. You inspire our minds to side with Kṛṣṇa and to accept the thoughts of the previous *ācāryas,* and you engage our senses in devotional service.

Being utilized by you is our experience of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Love for you is the dynamic force that can allow us to remain attached to the process of chanting the holy name, to *Kṛṣṇa-kathā* to taking *prasādam,* to the worship of Tulasī-devī, to remaining in the association of devotees, and to pushing on this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

We shall be able to cooperate amongst ourselves only if we love you. But how are we going to love you if we do not realize that you are the very sustenance of our spiritual life? It is due to your intense devotion to Kṛṣṇa and to your humble request to Him to engage us that we can maintain some dedication to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We can see no other cause but your sweet compassion.

So on this auspicious day we pray to Kṛṣṇa to grant us the greatest boon: attachment to your lotus feet. This attachment by far excels all other boons and includes them all. This attachment will lead us to understand that your association is eternal and will enable us to think of you constantly. This attachment will reveal to us the truth of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and unveil the mystery of how Kṛṣṇa expands Himself as the spiritual master.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the manifestation of Kṛṣṇa’s inconceivable potency, and you are Kṛṣṇa’s most intimate servant. Therefore you are the Supreme Personality of Servitor of Godhead. Please find some use for us in your service to Kṛṣṇa and maintain us as grains of dust at your lotus feet.

*Your servants in northwest Italy,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Madhusevita dāsa / ālī-Kṛṣṇa-devī dāsī

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Japan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I beg you to accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to the transcendental *saṅkīrtana* movement! All glories to transcendental book distribution! All glories to you!

Śrīla Prabhupāda! I’ve never seen you but I am your devotee. I listened to tapes of you chanting a few years before you left the planet. I was reading your books as well, but because I wan in compete darkness I couldn’t understand at all who was teaching this knowledge and for whom. But just one verse of this knowledge saved me from my material life and led me to Kṛṣṇa. All glories to transcendental knowledge of Lord Kṛṣṇa!

Śrīla Prabhupāda! You had complete faith in how book distribution can affect people. Therefore, being so compassionate to the people who were suffering because of spiritual ignorance, you offered your whole life to the mission of enlightening them with the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And your mission was successful. You have rescued so many people from the darkness of ignorance. Indeed, you are doing so even now and will do so forever.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! You said the hippies in New York had gotten something very important for their lives when you gave them the knowledge of Kṛṣṇa. And I did too. Because of your compassion, because of your mercy, because of your endeavor, I got something most important for my life and could become a devotee. I’ve never seen you or heard you personally, but I’m always seeing you in the form of your books and reading and listening to your books, which are your *vānī* and therefore as potent as you personally.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! You’re always shining like the sun. Wherever the sun is, there is no darkness. Actually, you’re the sun for us. And you are shining most when your books are printed. And when those books are distributed, the sun shines even more. There is no limit to your shining.

Just as the sun is glad to see the earth becoming satisfied in the spring, so your smiling is like that soft sunshine when you watch devotees taking *prasādam.* When you preach in your lectures, you’re just like the powerful sun in summer. When you give instruction, in talking with devotees or educating children, you’re just like pleasing moonlight in autumn. Just as moonlight gives taste to vegetables, you give us affection.

When you chastise your disciples, you’re like the severe sun in winter. You rescue your disciples by chastising them. Just as living entities cannot live without the sun, for us who are trying to be devotees your words are lifegiving, and we can’t neglect even one.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! Because of your causeless mercy, I’m a *saṅkīrtana* devotee. I heard that you smiled the most broadly when devotees distributed or printed your books. Your disciples and many of your senior granddisciples try to please you by distributing your books, and I also am doing so. Please let me help you by taking you, in the form of your books, to the fallen people so that they can listen to you. I beg you to please use me as you like.

(written by Mahātmā dāsa)

*Your servants in Japan,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Kavicandra Swami / Jagad-vīra dāsa

## Johannesburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

When the moon was born out of the Milk Ocean, the fish thought it was just another fish. They didn’t know what an extraordinary entity had appeared from within their midst. Similarly, the tendency is there, particularly for those of us who are materialistically minded, to not appreciate what a sublime personality you are and how significant your instructions are and how valuable it is to follow them.

A key to appreciating these things lies in a statement by Lord Brahmā in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (2.6.34):

*Na bhāratī me ‘ṇga mṛṣopalakṣyate*  
*Na vai kvacin me manaso mṛṣā gatiḥ*  
*Na me hṛṣīkāṇi patanty asat-pathe*  
*Yan me hṛdautkaṇṭhyavatā dhṛto hariḥ*

**“O Nārada, because I have caught hold of the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Hari, with great zeal, whatever I say has never proved false. Nor is the progress of my mind ever deterred. Nor are my senses ever degraded by temporary attachment to matter.”**

Because Brahmā has caught hold of the feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa so strongly and has such an affectionate relationship with Him, the Lord feels obliged to ensure that whatever Brahmā says comes true and that whatever he desires comes to pass. This is also why the Lord tells Arjuna in the *Bhagavad-gīta:* “Declare it boldly that My devotee is never vanquished.”

Kṛṣṇa didn’t declare it boldly Himself because He knew that some people would not believe Him, as sometimes He breaks His promise in order to help His devotee. However, if His devotee declares something or makes some prediction, He feels He has no alternative but to uphold His devotee’s word.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have said so many things and given so many instructions on how to practice and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. All we have to do is accept what you have said and apply it undeviatingly; then there is no doubt that we will have all success in all respects.

We have such a history of thinking we can come up with a “better way” to spread the movement, but this has all simply been a waste of time . All we ever managed to achieve through those methods was chaos and hardship. Our deviation from your order has its roots in our not appreciating the depth of your Kṛṣṇa conscious realizations and not understanding the depth of your relationship with the Lord. Or, in other words, it has its roots in our materialistic consciousness.

As you said, Kṛṣṇa consciousness is simple for the simple but very complex for those who make it so. You’ve given us everything: all we have to do is increase our ability to receive it. Your mercy is available to whoever will accept it, just like a sunshine. It’s not that there’s some clever trick involved. We simply have to be humble servants, understanding and applying your divine instructions, and we will be able to truly start realizing the nature and potency of your mercy, your divine grace, which transcends the limitations of time, space, and all the other constraints normally found in this realm. This mercy of yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda, carries within it the nondual nature of the spiritual world and the ś*acti* to place the submissive recipient in that nature and world.

What we witness in you in all your different manifestations is the relationships of a pure person with the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself. You are on the level of Prahlāda Maharaja. You are on the level of Śukadeva Gosvāmi or Arjuna. You are not a creature of this world. The Supreme Lord, who is the devotee of His devotees, is dedicated to you because of your unalloyed devotional love for Him. Of this there is no doubt.

What can we say or do? We should simply surrender at your lotus feet as minute particles of dust and pray for the intelligence to be submissive; we should try to cultivate the desire to simply be humble instruments for the fulfillment of your desires. Then we can be sure Kṛṣṇa will help us in all regards.

*Your humble servants in Johannesburg, South Africa,*

## Kṛṣṇadhāma Amsterdam-Holland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your tireless efforts to bless the fallen conditioned souls of this Kali-yuga, especially ourselves.

Service in separation is said to produce the highest experience of transcendental ecstasy, as exemplified by the *gopīs* and Lord Caitanya Himself. In many ways we, on our lowly level, find that separation from you heightens our realization of your unique qualities as we are forced to depend on you in ways we previously didn’t have to.

Previously you were personally present to resolve disputes or solve problems, and among your disciples there was certainly a great feeling of dependence on your abilities in this regard. Previously you were constantly preaching the message of *bhāgavata-dharma,* defeating atheists and establishing the philosophical basis of our attachment to the Absolute Truth.

Previously you were personally directing the development of your movement. You made the policies and devised the strategies for the preaching activities, and you set the priority—- the printing and distribution of your books as the focal point of our efforts to distribute the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And, of course, you were present in your personal form, displaying your transcendental beauty and your faultless standard of surrendered service to your spiritual master.

Your departure has forced us to come to a higher standard in our relationship with you. Gone is the personal association, the personal standard, the personal direction, which were like spiritual catalysts in our once-dull lives. Now, just in order to survive in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we have to realize that you are fully present in your instructions, in your mission, and in remembrance of your pastimes.

What to speak of just surviving, our experience is that when we absorb ourselves in these activities we see your potencies entering the hearts of your devotees, giving them the ability to practice and preach your message in a way they have never been able to before. Every day your influence is carries deeper into the heart of Kali, as more books go out and more conditioned souls are drawn toward this movement.

Separation from you certainly initially seemed like a very difficult step to take on our path of devotional service, but we are realizing now that, on the transcendental platform, it is not really separation but part of a lesson we have to learn to become fit to gain your association eternally.

*Your servants in Kṛṣṇadhāma (Amsterdam), Holland,*

Jaya Gopāla dāsa / Caitanya-Nṛsiṁha dāsa / Brahmarata dāsa / Rasācārya dāsa / Vātsalya dāsa / Badrāṇga dāsa / Śaraṇī-devī dāsī / Śravaṇā-devī dāsī / Rādhā-Gopīnātha-devī dāsī / Sarasvatī-devī dāsī / Kālindī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Claudette / Bhaktin Sandra

## Lagos

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We beg to offer our respectful obeisances at your self-effulgent lotus feet, which are the shelter of the whole world. By your utmost mercy we have been blessed with the *Śrī-Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana-yajña,* which was inaugurated by Lord Caitanya Himself. We won’t be able to repay you in this life or the next for all your manifold blessings on us.

The grandfather is more merciful than even the father. Therefore, on this, your glorious appearance day, we most earnestly appeal to you to empower us to assist our spiritual masters in spreading the holy names of the Lord to every town and village throughout the African continent.

*Jaya* Paraṁ-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Jagad-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda! *Jaya* ISKCON founder-*ācārya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your granddisciples in Lagos, Nigeria.*

## Laguna Beach

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Because you are so supremely devoted to your own spiritual master and because you are so highly intelligent and masterfully submissive to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, we endeavor to please you and pray that our efforts will be inspired by those same qualities. These are only a drop in the ocean of qualities that you have shown us and that you want us to inherit from you.

We pray only to become totally qualified to represent you and to be called your disciples and granddisciples.

Your mercy is there for everyone—- simply we must accept it and the result will be perfect.

Our goal in life is to simply take what you have given and reject the foolish “non-sense,” “no-science” conditioning that is the root of all fallen conditions of life.

As we drink of your ever-fresh and perfect mercy (which is identical to applying your instructions), we are constantly enlivened and supremely enthused to engage in this art of *bhakti-yoga.*

Before knowing you we were only the architects of our own suffering. Now, with the tools you have given us, we have the ability to be the architects of our own destiny.

You have bestowed countless gifts on us, Śrīla Prabhupāda; now we pray to deserve such gifts and to use them to remain in your service eternally. We can never do too much, endeavor long enough, or give up too much the false conceptions of “I and mine” to even begin to repay your kindness. We pray to remain in your service eternally, and we are eternally in your debt.

*Your servants in Laguna Beach, California.*

## Lansing

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

“This age of Kali will certainly be full of all activities of Kali, but this does not mean that the leaders of society, the executive heads, the learned and intelligent men, or above all the devotees of the Lord should sit down tightly and become callous to the reactions of the age of Kali. In the rainy season certainly there will be profuse rainfalls, but that does not mean that men should not take means to protect themselves from the rains.” *(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.17.28 purport)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our shelter from the onslaught of Kali, and on this transcendental occasion of your appearance, we extend our heartfelt gratitude to you. Outside of service to your mission, what reason is there to live? You gave us everything, including life itself. Searching in Kali’s domain for love, friendship, happiness, and wealth, we met with disappointment again and again. But after chewing the chewed, we turned to the process you gave us, and the higher taste prevailed, acting like an umbrella to keep out the rains of Kali. Unfortunately, we forget that you are our worthy object of love and our real friend. Despite our obstinacy and contaminated hearts, in so many ways we are seeing how you continue to maintain us within your ISKCON.

“Because the common man is unable to control the senses and the mind, it is his duty to seek the shelter of a great soul or a great devotee of the Lord and just try to please him. That will make his life perfect.” (*Bhag.* 3.22.6 purport)

It is our prayer that some day our service may be pleasing to you.

*Your eternal servants in Lansing, Michigan,*

Apūrva dāsa / Kamalinī-devī dāsī / Bhakta Dave

## Leicester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

The members of your growing Leicester *yātrā* family join together to offer you our most respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Bereft of any pure desire to love and serve Kṛṣṇa, we appeal to your all-merciful compassion, for you are clearly the brightest ray of Lord Caitanya’s benediction moon.

Whatever we think is ours, and whoever appears to be under our care, actually belongs to you. The ever-increasing decadence of the human race in every sphere of life proves man’s inability to produce anything of lasting value through his independent activities. By increasing our dependence upon you (your example, your instructions, and those devotees who follow you sincerely) we can and will break free from the utter futility of so-called independent living.

Over the past two years we have been endeavoring to build a new temple for your pleasure and the pleasure of Śrī Pañca-tattva. With your approval we would like to call it the Śrī Hare Kṛṣṇa Mandir. We hope that by the time this offering is read to you, the temple will have been completed and thus will become part of our offering.

By your mercy we have managed to complete the marble, wood, and brick work. The challenge is now up to us to give the temple life by following conscientiously in your footsteps: preaching vigorously, distributing your books, and expanding Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement in every way possible.

You’ve told us not to linger  
In this filthy, dirty place  
Of urine, blood and bile and stool  
And qualities debased.  
You’ve said our business should be done  
Without a waste of time,  
And we should quickly move along  
To something more sublime  
Without your causeless mercy  
In the form of clear instructions,  
Our lives would bear no fruitful gain,  
Just karmic repercussions.  
You are the king and we’re your slaves  
In this life and forever.  
We shall not think of feel or will  
Except to give you pleasure.

*Your low and unworthy servants in Leicester and Nottingham, U.K.*

## Lima

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet.

It is a source of great pleasure for us to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering for Your Divine Grace every year. But it is also a very difficult task, because as we try to describe your transcendental qualities and unparalleled grandeur, we feel insignificant and incapable of glorifying you as you deserve.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need to constantly be aware of the fact that it is simply because of your causeless mercy that we can advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It is only due to that mercy that we can hope to carry on this great movement without even a slight deviation from your instructions, thus maintaining everything exactly as you established it and left it upon your departure from this physical world.

A little deviation from your instructions now would mean great danger for the future of our movement. If we have faith that everything you taught us is perfect and complete, there will be no need to try something different. Only then will we able to preserve the pure Vaiṣṇavism you have so kindly and mercifully transmitted to us as a faithful representative of your Guru Maharaja and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

*Your eternal servants in Lima, Peru,*

## Lisbon

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Today we sat before the Tower of Belṁ, in Lisbon, to take our little lunch prasādam, we were thinking of how many ships had sailed from this very spot in the fifteenth century, sent by Prince D. Henrique, the “Holy Infant, “ in an attempt to find the sea passage to India. Other ships sailed from Sagres, in the south of Portugal.

We know whom they were really looking for in this endeavor, although they said they wanted to get spices and other things from India.

Of the first ships that were sent, some got lost and ended up in Brazil, and others didn’t get very far along the African coast—at best they got to the southernmost point in Africa. They called it the Cape of Good Hope because, they said when they got back to Portugal, they could almost see India. This was “Good Hope” in 1488.

We know whom they were very close to reaching from that point, although for them their “good hope” was to easily get to the seaports in India. They never knew that no seaport could provide a safe harbor in the ocean of birth and death.

So finally they reached the Indian coast in 1498 with three ships commanded by Vasco da Gama, and they came back to tell the world about the ocean passage to India and how to get rich by trading with the East.

They brought so much news and so many new things, as if they had found a new world, but they weren’t able to know Him or see Him, although He was already a youth by then.

Over the centuries, Portugal maintained colonies in India. In 1961 the last three colonies were given up: Goa, Daman, and Din (in Gujerat). So many things the Portuguese brought from India, but never did they bring the greatest treasure, His message: it was concealed from their eyes and ears. Besides, only you had the keys to the treasure chest.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Portugal is an unfortunate country, for it never got the mercy of seeing you or receiving the dust from your lotus feet throughout the twelve years of your transcendental travels in the West and around the world. Also, none of us Portuguese ever got the mercy of taking initiation from you. And the most unfortunate thing is that even up to the present, almost thirteen years after you went back to Kṛṣṇa, your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement still hasn’t been established on a sound footing in this country.

So we feel very much ashamed of this lack on our part to help establish your mission, and we beg for your forgiveness.

From the viewpoint of mundane history, this could have been the first Western Kṛṣṇa conscious country, but most unfortunately, it looks like it will be one of the last: we’re too much absorbed in the external energy.

So on this most auspicious day of your appearance anniversary, we bow down at your lotus feet and with all humility present before you our insignificant offering: the promise and desire to dedicate all our life and endeavors to solidly establishing this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement of yours in this little country and systematically taking the name and the glories of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, to all the Portuguese-speaking towns and villages.

Your mercy and potency are inconceivable, so who knows….. maybe Portugal can *still* become the first Kṛṣṇa conscious country in the West.

We prostrate ourselves at your divine lotus feet with all humility and respect.

*Your most insignificant servants in the Portugese yātrā,*

## Liverpool

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet.

It is said that by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa one gets *guru,* and by the mercy of *guru* one gets Kṛṣṇa. And this mercy is truly causeless mercy if we examine what qualification, or the lack of it, we have to be recipients. The causeless mercy of the spiritual master is an expression of his infinite compassion upon the fallen souls. There is no other reason.

How was it that in 1965 you appeared in the Western depths of Kali-yuga, a spiritual inversion bereft of any notion of God consciousness, a desert of suffering where the living entities are inflicting great pain upon themselves and each other. This is truly a miracle! In fact, it is the greatest miracle of this Kali-yuga. Lord Caitanya said, “One day My name will be known in every town and village.” And now Your Divine Grace has fulfilled that prediction. This planet is not aware of what you have done. You planted a spiritual time bomb that is slowly beginning to explode.

Our appreciation of your causeless mercy can only be expressed in our endeavor to execute the divine order of Your Divine Grace: to extend that mercy to others in the selfless preaching mood that you personified.

One day the world will stand still on this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, and huge stadiums will be filled with the resounding chants of *Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda!

*Your servants in Liverpool, U.K.,*

## London

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most glorious of days.

Within the last year we have celebrated the twentieth anniversary of the installation of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara. This has intensified our remembrance of you. It was you who also appeared here at this time, and it was because of your pure desire that Their Lordships manifested Themselves. It gives us great pleasure to report that the situation at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara Temple is becoming increasingly blissful. We are endeavoring to strictly follow all the instructions and guidelines you gave. Thus we are realizing that you are ever present here with us.

Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmi has declared that, of all the devotional activities, the five most important are worshiping the Deity, hearing *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* associating among the devotees, *saṅkīrtana,* and living in the holy *dhāma.* Due to your loyalty to his instructions, our lives are filled up with these activities. We can see that the atmosphere created by the performance of these activities on a daily basis has a profound effect on the consciousness of anyone who enters into this holy place. Therefore everyone is becoming completely satisfied. We are deeply grateful to you for this benediction.

We pray that you will bless us that we may continue, without cessation, to canvass among the fallen conditioned souls so that they may have the opportunity to receive this rarest of gifts. By so doing we will have the privilege of tasting the only nectar that is sweeter than the satisfaction of personally associating with yourself and your dear Lord—- that is, to bring others to that association.

*Begging to remain your menial servants,*

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for taking the order of your spiritual master as your life and soul, so that you brought the message of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya not only to the Western, English-speaking countries but also to the whole world, allowing even us, who are in an insignificant corner of the planet, to receive this message.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for having such unshakeable faith in the holy name of Kṛṣṇa that with unswerving determination and fixed conviction you delivered this matchless gift to *mlecchas* and *yavanas* such as ourselves, whom others would have rejected as unfit.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being so unlimitedly kind and causelessly merciful upon us, such undeserving, useless, miserable conditioned souls, that at an advanced age you selflessly sacrificed all material “comforts” like wife and family to bestow upon us the holy name of Kṛṣṇa, fearlessly facing impending death in utter dependence on Kṛṣṇa simply to deliver us from unimaginable suffering.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for devoting your time and energy to carefully training and nurturing your servants in the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Even though we have never personally met you, we can still appreciate you through the wonderful effect you had on the lives of those who associated with you and took you seriously.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for sending such sincere souls throughout the world to preach this message of Lord Caitanya, to do as you did, to follow in your footsteps, thus enabling us to receive your mercy in this tiny country in a remote corner of the planet.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for not only giving us the association of sincere devotees but also leaving us with sublime, transcendental books on the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness so we too may become your sincere servants.

Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we will be able to take the dust of your lotus feet upon our heads and become and forever remain your sincere servants. Thank you for mercifully allowing us this opportunity to try to glorify you on this most blessed day, your Vyāsa-pūjā.

*Your aspiring servants in Longdenville, Trinidad,*

## Malaga

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

This is our first offering on this very special day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, as the temple in Malaga, Spain, has been open only since August 1989. We would like to thank you for the infinite mercy you have shown us.

Your Divine Grace once told a story of a man who found a cane, a pumpkin and a wire and made a *vīṇa* out of these. You said that this was exactly what you did with us when you came to the West—taking *mlecchas* and *yavanas* and transforming them into devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa. You gave the highest meaning of life to the lowest persons. And this you did out of compassion, real compassion.

We devotees in this temple have recently gone through an experience like being shipwrecked after a storm. We all felt lonely and anxious, and our mind began deforming itself by speculation. Fear appeared, and we began searching for material protection, losing little by little the spiritual realization we had gained by your association through intense service.

But that is all in the past. Thank you again for the huge mercy you have shown us by bringing us together, inspiring us, and giving us this place, which is like Vaikuṇṭha. This temple relieves our anxiety, protects our realizations, liberates our mind, and gives meaning to our lives by setting us the goal of transforming it into a real center of spiritual knowledge that will help purify us and the entire southern part of the Spanish *yātrā.*

Please forgive us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for not being more intelligent in serving you, for not being more sincere, for not being more humble, and for not being the sort of devotees that you wish to have.

It is only by your grace that we have obtained this temple.

By your grace we feel inspired to engage in devotional service every day.

By your grace we have the arguments to conquer the atheists.

By your grace we have the power to convince the innocent to take up Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

By your grace we are able to tolerate reverses, conquer anger, and live in harmony.

We give infinite thanks to the Lord for sending such an intimate servant of His as you.

Your Divine Grace, you have shown us how to fight for Kṛṣṇa till the end of life.

Your Divine Grace, you have taught us how to see that everything belongs to Kṛṣṇa and how to engage everything in His service.

Your Divine Grace, by giving us confidence in Kṛṣṇa, you showed us that we don’t have to be afraid.

Your Divine Grace, you have taught us how to get a big vision of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You told us that the word *impossible* does not exist in the dictionary of the Vaiṣṇavas. And you showed us that by depending on Kṛṣṇa, we can attain anything.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, all the devotees of the temple here in Malaga promise you that, come what may, we will stay within the big family you have created, serving you and participating in the preaching as you wish us to. We also promise to work to increase the distribution of your transcendental books, as well as *prasādam*, and to increase our congregation.

Please cover us with the dust of your lotus feet so that we can feel inspired and protected.

*Your insignificant servants in Malaga, Spain,*

Dharma-putra dāsa / PaRāma-dharma dāsa / Sat-prema dāsa / Bhakta Matias / Bhakta Jose-Luis / Bhakta Juan / Mahā-bhāva-devī dāsī / Gāndharvikā-devī dāsī / Gokuleśvarī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Flor / Bhaktin Ausha

**Children**

Abhay / Upendra / Mahimaā

## Malaysia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. We here in Malaysia are the most neophyte granddisciples you have . We never really are able to value your *vāṇī* and your personal representatives.

By the steps of your lotus feet this land has become blessed so that we are able to distribute your books in three major languages—-Chinese, Tamil, and English—- and also in Telugu, Malay, and Malayali.

Only by your mercy is this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in Malaysia growing. By ourselves we are only good for quarreling among one another. We are victims of the age of quarrel. But we have the faith that by understanding your *vāṇī* properly we will be able to overcome that weakness.

You planted the seed of devotional service in Malaysia, and we are fortunate to have received it. We pray that you guide us daily to protect our devotional creeper from *anarthas.*

Please give us the mercy to understand how to serve you and not be a victim of the Kali-yuga’s laziness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive us for not being able to carry out your mission properly as you desire.

Please let us serve your lotus feet eternally.

*Your servants in ISKCON Malaysia.*

## Manchester

Dear Prabhupāda, your day once more,  
You give to us and yet we find,  
Within a bleak and blighted mind,  
Scant praise from out of hearts so poor.  
We pray your strength to stand and speak,  
True words which will be marked as true,  
Pure words which mark the truth of you,  
For we are worldly bound and weak.  
But lord, we pray that more than words,  
We find the strength to stand and show,  
In living proof, the truth we know;  
And how from you it has been heard.  
Once upon a time unmeasured,  
By any mortal means or thought;  
Whereby the soul forever sought  
You played and danced with your dear Lord.  
In love of endless excellence;  
His each desire your delight,  
His lover’s wish your life’s one light,  
In ever-present permanence.  
Where Kṛṣṇa is the Lord alone  
And all with all accord accept,  
Emotions that exceed respect,  
Behold but their beloved one.  
And where we were; O how we fell!  
Removed far from remembrance.  
What reason stole away our sense,  
That leaving there we chose this hell?  
When He whose knowledge is complete;  
Compassionate upon our plight,  
Considered that the time was right,  
That with His mercy we might meet.  
He then displayed His deep desire,  
To you and to your dearmost friends,  
Who all to execute His ends,  
Could come to this consuming fire.  
What otherwise would be the need,  
For one of perfect purity,  
Established in eternity,  
To reach this realm of lust and greed?  
Yet you and all saints such as this,  
Though here with us are far apart;  
The vision held within our heart  
Is ever abode of bliss.  
While we within the womb were placed,  
By the sensual force we served,  
As we desired and deserved;  
We were in ignorance abased.  
But before us your appearance,  
Is under higher energies;  
As and when the Lord does please,  
You came for our deliverance.  
All through the ages one by one,  
Aligned, great masters made their mark,  
Like beacon lights in deepest dark,  
Revealing our redemption.  
United all in single aim;  
For progressive revelation,  
Leading us toward salvation,  
By divine dictate they came.  
And you the last in that long line;  
That we can understand it well,  
The highest truth in you does dwell;  
No love more deep nor more sublime.  
O Prabhupāda we praise the day,  
That brought the blessing of your birth,  
The glory that you gave the earth,  
Grows greater with each passing day.  
Thus gathered here we celebrate,  
Though hardly have we eyes to see  
The truth of your identity;  
We pray may grace us, soon or late.  
The passage of a purest soul,  
So swift and yet so very deep.  
Fond memories that few can keep,  
May live today within us all.  
For form and substance are as one,  
Inside the heart of him who hears  
Your praises and with profound tears,  
Sees spirit, not separation,  
When we are with you, here or there;  
Connected in pure consciousness,  
Shall constant service show to us,  
That you in truth are ev’rywhere.  
But what is knowledge next to love?  
O lord! Your personality :  
Our individuality:  
Shall stand forever, far above.  
And so we bow before you now,  
And hold this day so very dear:  
A chance awaited all the years,  
That tender love for you may grow.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

With the onset of the terrible Kali age, we are seeing that all people are suffering unbearable miseries. It is getting worse and worse, and there is absolutely no respite other than the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Your movement is a swirling undercurrent of hope rising against a huge tide of ignorance and suffering. Sometimes it seems that we will also be overwhelmed, but when we remember your intense desire for the success of this movement, we know that Kṛṣṇa will be with us, if only because of your pure love,

To be your representative will not prove easy; it will be a task fraught with many difficult tests from within and without. Only the most sincere will be able to tolerate those tests and stand up for the truth you gave us.

We are not sincere; we will never be your true representatives, due to our determined desires for material enjoyment. But we pray to you today: please keep us in the association and shelter of those who are your sincere servants. If we become proud and offensive, ungrateful for that association, then punish us until we come to our senses. Whatever it takes to make us humble, dear Lord, please give us that. We only want to spend our lives working in your movement.

Falling prostrate at your feet,

*Your aspiring servants in Manchester, U.K.,*

## Mauritius Farm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Again, on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we thank you for engaging us all in your transcendental service. As the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* states*, mahat-servāṁ dvāram āhur vimukteḥ.* By rendering service to you, the greatest of *mahātamas,* and to your transcendental body, ISKCON, one opens the door to ultimate liberation, to the spiritual kingdom. Prahlāda Mahāraja recommends that we all follow at least one of these nine process of devotional service(ś*ravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ visnoh smaranam, etc.),* and the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* gives examples of how even the great *mahājanas* attained perfection by following just one of these nine processes. Now it has become so very easy to attain perfection, as we now have an ever-increasing wealth of your nectarean association by the good services of your BBT and Bhaktivedanta Archives: more than seven hundred lecture and conversation tapes and the books printed therefrom; all the book-transcription tapes themselves; what to speak of the *kīrtana, bhajana,* and video tapes of your lotus self, which are being profusely distributed, listened to and looked to and looked at, read, and profoundly appreciated all over the world.

Thus we can now be engaged twenty-four hours out of twenty-four, as even when we take rest we can play your nectarean tapes on an auto-reverse cassette-player and benefit from your divine association throughout our dreams. And when we chant *japa* along with your Vraja-like *japa* tape, O supreme spiritual master, all our miserable *maya* is completely defeated.

Then again, by the mercy of the Supreme Lord, your faithful GBC has defeated *māyā’s* infiltration of us and helped us to follow Ekādaśī, the mother of devotion (*mādhava-tithi bhakti-jananī),* along with other holy days, according to the true *tithi* observations.

By continuing in this way, we are confident that many moons will soon rise from among your disciples, granddisciples, and great-granddisciples. You wanted only one moon, one pure devotee, and you said you would then consider your mission a success, but we have no doubt that from all of those who have taken advantage of your most merciful and ever-expanding association, as abovementioned, there will soon be many, many moons rising, and maybe a few suns, too. We do not doubt this at all.

When we were with you in India, the “p*andyas”* would ask us what our *gotra* was, and you told us to say we were in the Gautama-gotra (your family- *gotra),* as we are all your spiritual sons and daughters. Therefore, we have no doubt of our liberation. When Lord Nṛsiṁha asked Prahlāda Mahārāja for a benediction, Prahlāda said, “Please liberate my demonic father.” The Lord replied, “What to speak of your father, fourteen generations of your family are liberated because you are a pure devotee.” Therefore, O beloved Guru Maharaja, we have no doubt of our liberation. You are the greatest of pure devotees, and we are part of your Prabhupādakula—- and very happy indeed to be so. Nārada Muni is famous for delivering one outcast named Mṛgāri, but you are liberating thousands of souls who are much worse than Mṛgāri.

O beloved Guru Maharaja, on this most holy of days, your blessed Vyāsa-pūjā, we clutch your lotus feet, crying, “O master, please, please keep us always situated in our eternal duty, service to you.”

(written by Arjuna dāsa)

*Your servants at the ISKCON Vedic Farm, Bon Accueil, Mauritius,*

## Mexico City

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances.. All glories to you.

When we simply think of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel sustained. We are here by your grace, and we are for you and with you.

Here in Mexico City the devotees and the friends of the devotees are fighting to keep the temple, which is your house. You came here in 1972 and 1975 to give your mercy—a pure saint, a perfect devotee of Kṛṣṇa.

From Venezuela you sent a letter to Hridayānanda Mahāraja: "The building is very appropriate for a temple and should be kept.” But now we are facing the danger of losing your temple, so we ask you to request Kṛṣṇa to help us keep this building.

We know that you are dear to the Lord, Śrīla Prabhupāda. But we are tiny, and we depend on you. We see that more and more people are taking your instructions to heart. We feel great transcendental pride to be your servants. We pray to be with you forever.

## Miami

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious occasion of your appearance in this world, we naturally reflect within our minds on the wonderful gifts you have left for us.

You have given us this wonderful ISKCON movement to help us go back to Godhead. This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is cent per cent spiritual, an extension of the eternal pastimes of Lord Caitanya. You knew that unless we could experience a spiritual taste higher and more exciting than our previous mundane experiences, we would have no chance to remain in this movement to become pure devotees. As we are struggling daily to purify ourselves, we can experience the wonderful adventure of becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious.

This movement has all the makings of a great adventure, just as in Kṛṣṇa-līlā and Caitanya līlā. In trying to spread this movement we must face powerful demons who would like to destroy us. We sometimes face opponents within our movement and even within our minds. Again and again we see that when we are almost defeated, Lord Kṛṣṇa steps in and reverses the situation, just as He defeated many demons in Vrandāvana. He purifies our own consciousness. He transforms our greatest apparent defeats into our greatest victories. In this way He intensifies our meditation on Him and our love for you. This is exactly the mood of the eternal *līlās* of Lord Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya.

In the spiritual world, whatever one desires becomes immediately fulfilled. Since this is a spiritual movement, if our desires are pure, then whatever we desire will become manifest. This movement is not bound by any mundane laws. If the Lord desires He can give us billions of dollars immediately. What is the difficulty for Him? If He desires, millions of devotees will join this movement. Yet we sometimes find ourselves bewildered by the problem of how to pay the temple’s bills or pay for multimillion-dollar lawsuits. We sometimes reduce our service to the Deities or eliminate some temple programs because too many devotees have left the movement. But Lord Kṛṣṇa is waiting to give us full facility to serve Him as soon as He sees that our desires have become pure.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have told us that as long as we continue to chant our rounds and follow the regulative principles, we will not have any financial difficulties. Why then do we have so many difficulties within this movement? If we look at ourselves, we will see how many of us are not keeping our vows. Lord Kṛṣṇa is eagerly waiting to give us the whole world—as soon as we follow your instructions.

Lord Kṛṣṇa secretly hid His Kaustubha gem within the treasury of Kāliyaā so that when the defeated Kāliya serpent was ready to worship the Lord with valuable paraphernalia, he would offer that most valuable gem to the Lord, thinking it to be his own. Similarly, earth’s unlimited hidden wealth all belongs to Kṛṣṇa, and by continuing to purify ourselves and the world by this *saṅkīrtana* movement, that wealth will eventually fall into the hands of sincere devotees, who will offer it back to the Lord by engaging it properly in His service. It remains there only for that purpose.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us a wonderful gift in the form of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. If we simply carry out your instructions and work cooperatively to spread this movement, we will relish excitement and bliss as we watch this movement expand by the Lord’s mercy. You have given us a golden opportunity to live very happy, exciting lives as we purify ourselves in preparation for going back home, back to Godhead. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your humble servants in Miami, Florida.*

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

We, your disciples and servants in Montreal, are feeling great separation from your divine person as we enter another decade of Kali-yuga. Thus, just as Arjuna remembered Kṛṣṇa’s teachings after the Lord’s departure form mortal vision, we are trying our very best to take shelter of your absolute instructions to mitigate our feeling of imcompleteness.

During your last visit here in 1975, as well as in a previous letter, you said, “ Kṛṣṇa has given you the intelligence to purchase this nice building. Keep on chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa and He will help you make it nice to attract the people. It should not be used just for your comfortable living. If side by side you can distribute my books, your temple will become glorious in Montreal. But where are the French books? Where are the French books?”

It’s an open secret that we are behind with the French publication of your books, especially in regard to the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta.* We therefore pray for your mercy, so that we may catch up on your transcendental literature. We know all too well that book publishing and distribution are your heart and soul. Please allow us to fulfill this desire of yours, lest we should miss out on this immortal nectar. Fortunately, we somehow managed to print one paperback in 1989. But how puny it seems compared to the stock of literature you have left us! You are like a second Vyāsadeva to us. And you have accepted us as your spiritual sons; yet, how far we are from being like Śukadeva! We are more like fishes on the sand in your absence. Please shower your rain of mercy on our desertlike city. You are our eternal lord and master. Kindly save us.

*Your devotees in Montreal, Canada.*

## Murāri-sevaka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Your disciples and followers at this remote outpost of Murāri-sevaka, your farm and temple in the beautiful hills southeast of Nashville, Tennessee, offer heartfelt obeisances in the dust at your lotus feet. We aspire to engage in your service forever, with no tinge of fruitive desire or mental speculation. Please bless us so that we may always remember your lotus feet and our identity as servants of the servants of your holy lotus feet.

*Your servants at Murāri-sevaka, Tennessee.*

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

While praying to Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva, Prahlāda Maharāja describes the mood of an extremely advanced devotee of the Lord. He said that there are many saintly persons very much interested in their own deliverance form the sufferings of material existence. For this purpose they give up their material life and go to the secluded forest to meditate with a vow of silence. They have no faith or interest in altruistic activities—delivering others from suffering. And hence they don’t come to the cities and towns.

Prahlāda Maharaja further says, “ But I don’t wish to be liberated alone, leaving aside all these poor fools and rascals. I know that without taking shelter of Your lotus feet they cannot be happy. Therefore I wish to bring them back to the shelter of Your lotus feet.” This concern for others constitutes the most sublime way of worshiping Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you possess every conceivable transcendental quality, all of which flow from your unalloyed and unmotivated spontaneous love for the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa. When we think about you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the context of Prahlāda Maharāja’s prayer—-that he would prefer to remain in the midst of the hellish cities and towns and help them become Kṛṣṇa conscious—we conclude that you are a real representative of Prahlāda Maharaja.

Other sages are far inferior to you because they lack the compassion to deliver the poor people suffering in this world of birth and death. You left your transcendentally comfortable and peaceful situation in Vṛndāvana and risked everything, even your life, for the sake of the people suffering perpetually in the illusory existence of *māyā.* Your coming to the Western countries is like a person’s entering in the flames of blazing fire to rescue some helpless child surrounded in every direction by the flames.

Your concern and mercy for suffering humanity were boundless, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Of course, it is obvious that we cannot appreciate even a fragment of your mercy. You gave perfect directions for humanity. History has rarely seen a personality of your caliber, so filled with compassion for the poor living entities.

You underwent all hardships to found and organize ISKCON so that this mood of preaching and distributing mercy in the form of Kṛṣṇa consciousness might continue for at least ten thousand years, if not forever. Your ISKCON is but a manifestation of your mercy, dedicated exclusively to distributing Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which is the only way to obtain supernatural pleasure, according to Prahlāda Maharaja.

There is another very special aspect of spiritual life that you have clarified: that preaching is the essence of all spiritual activities. This is the best way to worship the Supreme Lord. The criteria of a *mahātamā* are not meditation in a secluded place and endeavor for personal liberation. They are, instead, his faith in Kṛṣṇa and his enthusiasm for preaching and helping people become Kṛṣṇa conscious, even at the risk of personal inconveniences. The mood of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness is your unique contribution to the world. And for that the world is ever indebted to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious anniversary of your appearance day, we collectively beg at your feet for the strength and faith to cultivate Kṛṣṇa consciousness the way we are supposed to.

We beg to remain your insignificant servants.

*Your servants in Nagpur, India,*

Svayaṁ-bhagavān dāsa / Vīrabhadra dāsa / Haricaraṇa dāsa / Vrajabhūmi dāsa / Sudāmā dāsa / Vidura dāsa / Rādhā-Rāmaṇa dāsa

## Nava-Jiyaḍa-Nṛsiṁha-Kṣetra

**Perfect Vision**

As a young child, spending hours gazing  
*At Lord Kṛṣṇa’s vigraha from in the neighborhood temple—*  
*Where others perhaps saw one of many gods,*  
*And then only as symbolic representation—*  
*You saw your worhipable Lord, the son of Nanda,*  
*Smiling sweetly back at you.*  
*When in school the Christian teacher*  
*Presumptuously dismissed the notion of karmic reaction,*  
*You could see that he was wrong….*  
*Soon you would obtain the weapons of knowledge*  
*From your eternal master*  
*To defeat all such speculations.*  
*Although others may have thought,*  
*“He is a nice sādhu,” you saw in Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta*  
*Sarasvatī the carrier of Lord Caitanya’s message—*  
*You say your eternal preceptor.*  
*When others saw in his order to preach in the West*  
*Little more than a disturbance in their daily sādhana—*  
*You saw the order of Lord Caitanya*  
*And made it your life and soul.*  
*When everyone imagined a need to raise India’s economic standard,*  
*To follow the wasteland footsteps of the industrial West—*  
*You saw them drinking poison and*  
*Sought to fend them from their foolishness.*  
*When others saw no need for more than a simple magazine,*  
*Printed in small quantities for a small mission—*  
*You envisioned a publication on a par with the best color periodicals,*  
*For mass distribution—- a viable alternative to all mundane news.*  
*Where others saw wild, untameable hippies*  
*In the Lower East Side and Haight- Ashbury—*  
*You recognized the best candidates for receiving the māhā-mantra,*  
*Your dancing white elephants to take India and the world by surprise.*  
*Where others took skyscrapers, rockets, and United Nations*  
*As signs of advanced civilization,*  
*You deemed it all as cheap glitter decorating a rotting body—*  
*A shell void of brain, transporting passengers swiftly to hell.*  
*When others saw chanting the name of God as a sectarian religion and*  
*Wanted something “simpler, more acceptable,” for average people—*  
*You saw no reason to change the perfect message*  
*Of the unbroken line of ācāryas.*  
*When the hippies and young devotees viewed Lord Jagannātha*  
*And took part in the kīrtana, considering it all great fun—*  
*You saw them as dedicated though yet unrefined,*  
*Seeming to come straight from Vaikuṇṭha.*  
*When journalists and lawyers got mileage with the term*  
*“brainwashing”*  
*to describe the threat of preaching—*  
*you saw it as appropriate, but took the direct meaning:*  
*“First we give you brain, then wash it clean of māyā.”*  
*And in Māyāpur, where disciples saw only farmland—*  
*You saw the place of realizing Bhaktivinoda Thākura’s vision,*  
*A city to draw the attention of the world*  
*To Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the place of His divine appearance.*  
*When your BBT staff thought printing two books a month impossible—*  
*You saw the chance to prove that for one who has the blessings*  
*Of guru and Kṛṣṇa, “Impossible’ is a word in a fool’s dictionary.”*  
*When it was night and all were sleeping—*  
*You saw the best time to write your books,*  
*Delivering to the world the perfect vision*  
*Of one who is not of this world.*  
*So when others see us as “blindly following,”*  
*We have to agree:*  
*Those who are blind do well to follow the one*  
*Whose sight is clear.*  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have perfect vision*  
*Because you see Lord Kṛṣṇa at every step.*  
*We simply pray that we may always follow behind you, hearing your*  
*Steady pace, and ever receive your perfectly merciful glance.*

(written by Kṛṣṇa-kṣetra dāsa)

*Your servants in Nava-Jiyaḍa-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra (in the Bavarian Forset, Germany),*

## New Dwārakā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Each year we gather together here in New Dwārakā, and while seated at your lotus feet and before the radiant forms of your beloved Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhiśa, we make some humble attempt to offer you words of praise on your Vyāsa-pūjā. Hopefully our words will come close to adequately describing your position as one of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s dearmost devotees.

It is simultaneously an impossible and yet highly relishable endeavor, one that always seems to reveal to each and every one of your disciples and granddisciples how much harder we will all have to continue to work in order to become even a mere fraction of the devotees and disciples that you want us to be.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, everything you have given us is wonderful—- your *kīrtanas* are wonderful, your classes are wonderful, your pastimes are wonderful, your lifelong example is wonderful, your books with all your realizations are wonderful, your orders are wonderful, your devotees are wonderful, your worshipful Deities are wonderful, your sense of humor is wonderful, your *prasādam* is wonderful, and this chance to celebrate your divine appearance in this world year after year, and what will become century after century, is also wonderful. We can only thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, again and again. For whereas the nondevotees are forced to hear and repeat the stale pastimes of their materialistic heroes and attempt to find some tiny pleasure therein, we have been blessed by you with the ever-increasing bliss associated with hearing and chanting the glories of the Lord and His devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will go down history as one of the most courageous and radical revolutionaries the world has ever witnessed. At the advanced age of sixty-nine, and with little or no encouragement from any of your Godbrothers, you crossed the ocean with great difficulty, and upon arriving here in America you boldly planted the Vaiṣṇava flag of Kṛṣṇa consciousness here in the West. Then you declared America to be the property of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and all of her inhabitants to be long-lost devotees.

“If they knew what I was up to, they would kill me,” you once said. How true! However, you left before they could even consider it, Śrīla Prabhupāda. And now, in your physical absence, there has been some attempt on their part to kill your movement. Obviously they are not aware of who they are dealing with. Their efforts to harass, tease, or even destroy your movement will only reveal how totally alive and in control you are and how much Kṛṣṇa is controlled by your love for Him. Someday, perhaps, we shall get the chance to express our gratitude to all of these rascals who have inadvertently given us the chance to serve in this fight. Śrīla Prabhupāda, unlike your Godbrothers, you were able to cut through the passion and ignorance of our country and deftly marry the vast wisdom of the *Vedas* with the technology and wealth of the West. And, as in any good marriage, “What God had put together let no man put asunder.” So the futile attempts on the part of some foolish materialists to send this movement “packing” whence it came will only backfire in their faces and result in a greater appreciation for the authenticity of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement; the Vedic literatures; the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa; His most recent incarnation, Lord Caitanya; and his foremost devotee in recent history, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Like all your devotees throughout the world, Śrīla Prabhupāda, today we would like to again commit ourselves as individuals, and collectively as a community, to increase our efforts to become serious and fixed-up devotees. In this way all of your transcendental dreams for a Kṛṣṇa conscious Los Angeles may one day be realized. We pray to you to protect us here, for the influence of the material energy can be very great. The greatest protection is the chanting of the holy name, and we pray sincerely that each and every one of us can become more and more attached to the Lord’s benevolent shelter, which He so freely offers through the recitation of His name.

A devotee in England, Tribhuvanātha Prabhu, once recounted an amazing story form your pastimes. He was the newly appointed temple president at the Bhaktivedanta Manor back in the early 70’s. You arrived there one day and were immediately escorted to your quarters on the second floor. It was cold and damp in England at that time of the year, so Tribhuvanātha lit a fire in the fireplace to take away some of the chill. Then he left the room for some time in order to see to some other affairs, and you took up your beads. While sitting behind your low desk you very softly began to chant *japa.* Moments later a very anxious and excited devotee came running to find Tribhuvanātha. “Prabhu, come quickly, there’s a great deal of smoke billowing out from under the door of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s room!” Tribhuvanātha ran upstairs, tore open the door, and fought his way through the smoke-filled room. Evidently, he had not opened the chute in the chimney, and consequently none of the smoke could get out. He opened the chute and ran and opened the windows and doors, and this helped to alleviate the situation. Then he went over to you, his eyes full of tears from the smoke, and offered obeisances and humbly apologized for all the smoke and commotion. Your eyes were closed and you were still chanting, and after he spoke you opened your eyes, looked around, and said, "Huh? Smoke? Oh. Yes-that’s all right.” As if you hadn’t even noticed.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this entire material world is nothing more than a smoke-filled forest fire! But armed with your deep faith in Kṛṣṇa’s name, you moved about unaffected. Traveling from country to country, you delivered the panacea for Kali-yuga, the *mahā-mantra,* without a tinge of desire for profit, adoration, or distinction. Yet amazingly, you gained the greatest profit—the satisfaction of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta- and the greatest distinction as the “savior of the world,” and ultimately the adoration of millions of thankful and indebted devotees for the next ten thousand years.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep us always close to you by engaging us somehow or other on your service so that year after year and life after life we will continue to gather at your lotus feet on this auspicious day to hear and marvel at your glories and express our love and eternal indebtedness to you.

*Your eternal servants in New Dwārakā, Los Angeles,*

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Upon reading some of the offerings made in previous years by my Godbrothers, I am becoming increasingly embarrassed by my complete lack of devotion to you, and I am feeling completely unqualified to place my offering amongst those of my exalted Godbrothers.

You have stated that you can judge the potency of a preacher by how many devotees he has made. I understand that you mean pure devotees in this context, not immature neophytes. As you have often told us, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura stated that he would be satisfied if he could make one pure devotee, who he likened to the sun or moon in comparison to the stars, because of the ability of the stars to provide more than token illumination.

You have made many pure devotees. Some immature Vaiṣṇavas, in their attempt to involve themselves in political intrigue, tell us that there are “no pure devotees in the movement at present.” This is indirectly a criticism of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. They are indirectly stating that your mission is a failure. Nothing could be further from the truth.

Not only have you made many pure devotees during your manifest pastimes on this earth, but many pure devotees associated of yours have taken birth just to assist you.

Most of your Godbrothers did not recognize your exalted position because of their own lack of insight. Similarly, some of your disciples do not recognize the purity of their Godbrothers.

This is not at all surprising. Even during Kṛṣṇa’s presence on earth five thousand years ago, many persons refused to acknowledge His greatness and engaged in harsh criticism. Take Śiśupāla, for example. He objected to Kṛṣṇa’s being the first person worshiped at the Rājasūya sacrifice. He offensively stated: “I think Kṛṣṇa is no better than a crow.” Of course, he was killed for such an offense.

We should not allow our ears to hear offenses against our Godbrothers. Even if one is not the direct offender but quietly sits and tolerates while offenses are being made, he is culpable.

This is illustrated in the story of the Dakṣa-yajña, where Lord Śiva was insulted. Even though Dakṣa’s *brāhmaṇa* associates were not envious of Śiva, as Dakṣa was (you state this in your purport), they were still held responsible for Dakṣa’s offenses and had to suffer severe consequences.

Similarly, when Draupadi was insulted by Duryodhana and company, others, such as Grandfather Bhīṣma and Droṇācārya, who did not interfere with the insult, were held responsible.

I am praying, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you will allow me to have a service attitude toward your many exalted disciples. Because of the presence of so many pure devotees, there is great hope for the future of this movement and for your granddisciples.

If I can become the servant of your pure devotee disciples—-or better yet the servant of the servant of your pure devotee disciples—- that will be the perfection of my life.

Please forgive me for all the offenses I have committed against your great disciples by not properly defending their exalted position.

(written by Bīr Kṛṣṇa dāsa Goswami)

## New Gokula (Brazil)

*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our cry:*  
*We’ve all fallen within this material sky.*  
*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our history,*  
*Which is so old that it is lost in memory.*  
*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our case:*  
*The bhakti path you’ve given us by Kṛṣṇa’s grace*  
*Will save us all from māyā’s embrace.*  
*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our will:*  
*We’ve all been in the saṁsāra spinning wheel*  
*Life after life, now please make it stop still.*  
*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our claim:*  
*We were all chewing the chewed without any shame;*  
*But by Kṛṣṇa’s desire, in the middle of Kali-yuga you so kindly came*  
*And freely distributed Lord Caitanya’s holy name.*  
*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our plea:*  
*By your unlimited mercy you’ve made us all see.*  
*And by our inconceivable good fortune you’ll set us all free*  
*From all material entanglements without any fee.*  
*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, please listen to our lamentations:*  
*We’ve all been bound by millions of illusions,*  
*Entangled in a karmic network of sinful reactions,*  
*Wandering blindly in the darkness of ignorance ‘s night.*  
*But you’ve opened our darkened eyes to give us some sight*  
*And empowered us to see the eternal and blissful spiritual light.*

*Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, you’re so right!*

*Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!*

*Your servants at the Nova Gokula Farm Community, Pindamonhangaba, Brazil,*

## New Govardhana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day we are awarded the opportunity to reflect upon your glories by offering our obeisances unto you and thinking of you. In this way we not only feel great bliss and satisfaction, but we also destroy many great impediments on our path back to Godhead. Of course, you are the one who purifies us. This purification is good for our souls, so let us dive deep into your glories. To have the opportunity to meditate upon you, to engage in your service, to disregard our whimsical notions of happiness and pleasure, to chant the *mahā-mantra* and assist your movement—- all are great benedictions. We at New Govardhana, joining with your devotees everywhere, offer our heartfelt thanks to you on this great day. We pray that with renewed enthusiasm and vigor we will render some service that is some use to you; otherwise we are worse than useless.

*Your servants at New Govardhana (Murwillumbah, Australia),*
