## New Jagannātha Purī (Brazil)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, eternally.

We pray simply to serve you constantly and permanently. You so mercifully delivered to us your books, which are transcendental literature. Your message clearly reveals you to be a pure and perfect devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. By your mercy we can understand that the qualities of a Vaishnava are real. Love, compassion, humility, magnanimity, and numerous other qualities are fully manifest in you.

We are very grateful to Your Divine Grace for giving us the opportunity to practice the self-realization process for this age: the chanting of the holy names of the Lord. As we sing every morning,

*Mahāprabhoḥ kīrtana-nṛtya-gīta-*  
*Vāditra-mādyan-manaso rasena*  
*Romāṇca-kampāśru-taraṅga –bhājo*  
*Vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“Chanting the holy name, dancing in ecstasy, singing, and playing musical instruments, the spiritual master is always gladdened by the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Because he is relishing the mellows of pure devotion within his mind, sometimes his hair stands on end, he feels quivering in his body, and tears flow from his eyes like waves. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master.”**

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we hope to become worthy servants of your devotees.

*Your eternal servants at Nova Jagannātha Purī, Morretes, Brazil,*

Aṣṭvarga dāsa / Vedavyāsa dāsa / Acārya dāsa / Bhakta Mogra / Bhakta Eduardo / Bhaktin Sonia

**Children**

Bhūmi / Gopī

## New Māyāpura

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who are most merciful.

We should spend every moment of our lives in glorifying your divine self, because it is only due to your causeless mercy that we can get even a drop of the Lord’s mercy. So what to speak of glorifying you on the very auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

In the future, millions of souls will try to understand the transcendental character of your divine personality and the simplicity of your instructions: “Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy,” “Simple living and high thinking,” etc.

Although our society in France had to cross some difficulties, because the devotees went in trying to follow your instructions more seriously, people are becoming more favorable and more convinced of the importance of your movement. Only by practicing very sincerely the process you gave us will we be able to fulfill the goal of your mission. And the way by which future generations of devotees will appreciate your divine message is through the example given by the present generation.

By your causeless mercy we have the opportunity to live here in New Māyāpura Dhāma, at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Lakṣmī-Narasiṁhadeva, Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, Śrī Śrī Giri-Govardhana, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda-Mādhava. It is at the same time a way of living and a way of preaching. The difficulties we went through here were due to forgetfulness of your instructions, and we are eager now to fulfill your desire and develop this project as you would like.

When you came here and saw all the guests and devotees outside taking *prasādam,* you started to cry, and you said, “In the future thousands of people will come here to take shelter of this place.” We have to show them how to live a simple and spiritual life and offer everything to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda-Madhava.

Already we are developing a program of seminars so that the most intelligent people in this country will become attracted to participate in your extraordinary movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by living the way you want us to, we can feel your presence and guidance. We have to preach by example and distribute your books.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this very auspicious day, we pray at your lotus feet that you empower us to develop this project of New Māyāpura so we can show the world how sublime your teachings are and also inspire everyone to become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Please let us have a drop of your sincerity, determination, enthusiasm, and patience. Then it is sure that this project will flourish. Let us also work together with all the other devotees in your ISKCON movement so that your wonderful mercy will inundate the entire world.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Kindly bless us so that we can please you with our service and always remain engaged at your lotus feet.

*Your humble servants at New Māyāpura Dhāma, Lucay-le-Male, France.*

## New Orleans

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

On this blessed day we celebrate your divine appearance, which transformed the whole world from a dark hole of sinful activities into a place of hope where people have the chance to go back home, back to Godhead.

We can see that those who serve you the best let their actions speak for them, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Those devotees who are doing your bidding by continually preaching the holy names and distributing your books all over the world must be regarded as your emissaries. We pray for their holy association, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We pray that we may be able to cooperate to serve you offenselessly. We pray that we can become austere and qualified to receive your matchless gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We pray for your blessings to help us follow the process of *sādhana-bhakti* as you prescribed it for our benefit. We pray for you to help us chant sixteen rounds nicely and read your books every day. We pray that one blessed day we may become fully qualified members of this world *saṅkīrtana* party of preaching named ISKCON, which you inaugurated to fulfill the desire of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmi, the previous *ācāryas,* and Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. We pray that we may maintain this New Orleans temple of yours for the service of the Deities(whom you said were so beautiful) and for your mission of preaching in the New Orleans area, and not for our sense gratification.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep us under the shelter of your lotus feet.

(written by Catur-bāhu dāsa)

*Your humble servants in New Orleans,*

**Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Catur-bāhu dāsa

## New Rāman Reti

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances, and please accept this insignificant poem in your honor.

The bona fide spiritual master  
Is the perfect person God does send  
To facilitate our desire  
To go back to Him again.  
By God’s supreme mercy,  
We cannot be bereaved:  
If we fully want to surrender to Him,  
He sends us all we need.  
His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda  
Is the perfect answer to a sincere soul’s desire.  
The movement he created, to save the degraded,  
Has in it all the blessings we require.  
His Divine Grace declared that Kṛṣṇa is for everyone,  
That Kṛṣṇa consciousness is possible in a moment,  
That “impossible “ is a word in a fool’s dictionary—-  
In fact by his own life he had shown it.  
Who would have believed a tiny old man  
Could spread Kṛṣṇa’s name to every nation?  
How can we explain the miracles in our lives?  
It was only Prabhupāda’s faith and dedication.  
Prabhupāda’s full faith in Kṛṣṇa’s name—-  
That He could do anything at all—-  
Was that carried him to us, so lost and vain,  
And attracted us to him to heed his call.  
‘Jesus is our guru,’ Prabhupāda said,  
and Jesus said that faith can lift a mountain,  
and Lord Kṛṣṇa said it’s our doubts that are demons,  
and by our doubting all our miseries will be mounting.  
So he made this society to nurture our faith,  
And he said that this society is him,  
And he said there is no advancement we can make  
By leaving his ISKCON, his ship above all sin.  
So it’s humility that will help us in his ISKCON,  
And it’s pride alone that will make us leave—-  
The unwillingness to be the servant of his servant,  
Which Lord Caitanya said is what we must be.  
To hear Kṛṣṇa’s names with his disciples,  
Prabhupāda said, was the best medicine he could find.  
So let us spread Prabhupāda’s teachings all together,  
And upon us Prabhupāda will be kind.  
Nothing can act, nothing can move, without Kṛṣṇa’s desire;  
This is Kṛṣṇa consciousness, Prabhupāda said,  
And one who accepts all that happens as directly Kṛṣṇa’s mercy  
Can alone surrender to Him and go back home to Godhead.  
So with all the humility we have at our command,  
We pray to be the servants of your servants.  
Please, birth after birth just let us know our worth,  
As tiny particles of your mercy.

*(*written by Anubhāvānanda dāsa)

*Your servants at the New Rāman Reti farm in Alachua, Florida.*

## New Tālavān

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

The disciples of Śrī Rāmānujācharya, fearing his imminent departure from this world, prayed to him in this way: “Your body, which is ever engaged in the service of the Lord, can never be material. Our bodies are certainly temporary, but you exist only on the platform of spiritual life. Therefore, our request is that we never be deprived of the sight of your transcendental form.”

Taking pity upon them, Śrīla Rāmānuja accepted his disciples’ request and ordered that expert sculptors be brought there to carve a stone *mūrti* in his likeness. After three days the work was complete. Then the *mūrti* was bathed with water from the Kaverī River and installed upon a newly constructed altar. Śrīla Rāmānuja then came slowly up to the *mūrti* and filled it with his potency by breathing into the crown of the head.

Turning to his disciples, he said, “ This is my second self. When I have cast off this body, you may worship this form instead of me.”

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are with us eternally in your instructions, When we simply pick up one of your transcendental books, our consciousness is purified by its words, which in actuality are directly emanating from your lotus mouth. As long as we cooperate lovingly to spread your glories and the glories of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu throughout the three worlds, our eyes will never be bereft of the sight of your glorious form.

We pray to you to please allow us to use our sense only to serve and glorify you and your devotees with great vigor, and therefore life after life, in heaven or in hell, we will be blissfully engaged in fulfilling your loving instructions.

*Your servants at the New Tālavān farm, Carriere, Mississippi.*

## New Vrajamaṇḍala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your divine feet on this auspicious day of your appearance anniversary.

Today, as we celebrate your appearance day, we wish to express our deepest gratitude for this magnificent opportunity you give us each year to glorify you by writing a Vyāsa-pūjā homage. In this way we can concentrate all our attention on your divine personality. Although many years have passed since you were physically present, we feel near to you due to the link we have with your divine mission. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have been very charitable to accept so many fallen souls as your disciples and engage them in your service.

To take shelter of you is like being born a second time. As a parent protects and trains a child, so with enormous tolerance and love you protect us and train us to act on the spiritual platform.

Let us remember you in all the acts of our lives, and not only on certain occasions. You have given us the tools needed to bring this material existence to an end. Although you are not physically present, we know you will continue to lead us by means of your divine instructions.

To see you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is a mystic experience, because you come from Kṛṣṇaloka. The hearts of all the devotees melt and tears come to their eyes when they experience your compassion for them. You have published the most beautiful books, and your wonderful pastimes provide sustenance for all the devotees as they push on your *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we implore you again: Please bless us and renew our enthusiasm to serve your lotus feet for all eternity.

*Your servants in New Vrajamaṇḍala, Brihuega, Spain,*

## Newcastle

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your wonderful ISKCON movement, which is the main branch of the Caitanya tree and the shelter for all fallen souls in the Kali-yuga.

We bowdown at your lotus feet and beg that by your mercy we may continue to have the wonderful privilege of service within your society of devotees.

Your small temple here in Newcastle is now thriving. From our small beginnings with a handful of guests, we have watched attendance grow, and it is so inspiring to see many people coming, eager to partake of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement that you have created. Our joint meditation is to increase all the projects that we are doing more and more: *harināma,* Food for Life, preaching in the schools and universities, and distribution of your transcendental literature. This we may achieve only by your continued mercy.

Thank you for finding so many different ways for all of us to be of some assistance in spreading the glories of the holy names to every town and village.

*Your servants at ISKCON Newcastle,*

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Divyamiṁha dāsa / Jagad-vāsi dāsa / Bhakta Brian / Bhakta Dave / Bhakta Stephen / Hlādinī-śakti-devī dāsī

## North Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmi, you are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Westrern countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

The benefits of your transcendental association, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are unfathomable.

*‘sādhu-saṅga,’ ‘sadhu-saṅga’—sarva-śāstrekaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

**“The verdict of all reveled scriptures is that by even a moment’s association with a pure devotee, one can attain all success.” (Cc.** ***Madhya*** **22.54)**

By Kṛṣṇa’s mercy we are still able to gain your transcendental association in so many ways. Your books continue as a reservoir of illumination for our dull minds. Your *guru-pūjā* is a daily source of joy for all who participate. Your instructions on the multifarious activities of devotional life are readily available in your books and letters and audio and video recordings. Every time we read the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* we can easily understand that you have given us everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the perfect example, the perfect teacher, and the perfect representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Pleased by your pure devotion, Lord Kṛṣṇa directly instructed you how to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world. The modern technological society is certainly a curse. But as Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee, you had the power to transform that curse into a benediction. Now, in such a short time, Kṛṣṇa conscious literature has spread to every town and village all over the planet. You used every available facility to enable event the most fallen to engage in the Lord’s service. However, like a beautiful lotus flower poised above the water, you always remained completely unaffected. *Mṛdaṅga* and *karatālas, rice, dāl, capātīs,* and *sabjī;* simple living and high thinking. This was your standard—- pure, simple, beautiful, spiritual.

Before you left us you said that you thought your disciples were prepared and equipped to carry on your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement without you. That was simply your humility, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Please don’t leave us alone with this great responsibility. Without you we feel lost, without hope. The *śāstra* states that the *guruṣ* mercy (and with it the ability to carry out his orders) is always available to the sincere disciples. Lacking all sincerity, I simply weep in shame. Please don’t abandon this drowning soul. Please continue to inspire us from within to take up your mood of renunciation and dedication to this mission.

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* Dadhīcī Muni was approached by the demigods to surrender his body for the higher cause of producing a thunderbolt to kill the demon Vṛtrāsura. In your purports you very wonderfully explain the urgent need of the devotee to surrender his life for the higher cause of spreading this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. After all, you explain, the body must be given up sooner or later.

If we—your disciples, granddisciples, and uninitiated followers—can offer anything to you on this most auspicious day, let it be our renewed dedication to unswervingly preach the message of Lord Caitanyadeva under the shelter of your lotus feet..

Begging to remain your servants eternally,

*The devotees of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulananda in North Sydney and at the New Gokula farm,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Northern Ireland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

While the world is drowning in an ocean of sense gratification, and a bewildered materialistic civilization, having abandoned all values and regard for the sacredness of life, is drifting toward destruction, you are the light that has come into this world to reestablish *dharma,* following in the footsteps of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhantā Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, who spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over India and instructed you to spread Kṛṣṇa’s name all over the world.

What hope is there for this world but the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa, and what chance have people to chant it unless they understand the saving grace of Kṛṣṇa’s name by carefully studying your books, the matchless gifts you have given to humanity—-“People in this age shall get light from this *Purāna”?*

It is up to us to be an instrument in your hands to SAVE THE WORLD NOW by following in the footsteps of your disciples and granddisciples who, throughout these years since you left us physically, have not budged an inch from their determination to distribute your books very widely. Indeed, these disciples have brought about some sort of “revolution in the consciousness of men,” as is revealed by a simple look at the news coming from certain rapidly changing areas of the world.

We are able to bring you the good news on this Vyāsa-pūjā day that by the power of book distribution we have been able this past year to drive out the *māyā* that had entered in the form of paraphernalia sales, and we are happily experiencing the truth of your promise that everything will increase when books are the basis.

Please bestow your mercy on us so that we will be able to participate more fully in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Kṛṣṇa CaitanyaMahāprabhu by following the example of your exalted book distributor servants. In this way we hope to drown this part of the world in the ecstatic ocean of your book distribution.

*Your servants at Hare Krishna Island, Northern Ireland,*

## Padova

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Dear spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us Kṛṣṇa; therefore we are eternally indebted to you for this gift. Unfortunately, we have not yet developed this understanding, and therefore we are always trying to repay our so-called debts to the material nature, and we are repeatedly frustrated. Actually, you are the only one whom we can trust, in whom we can place all our faith, to whom we can surrender our lives completely, without hesitation.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you never disappoint anyone who comes to you for shelter. The material nature, however, gives us disappointment and no shelter at all. Still, we are very hopeful of getting something from her, even though she is always kicking us. Ever hopeful, we tolerate and forgive all the miseries she inflicts on us.

By your example you taught us how we can elevate ourselves to the stage of goodness if we simply stick to the principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness without devṇation and serve Kṛṣṇa by preaching His mission everywhere. In the Western countries we have received a so-called education, and therefore we habitually take everything cheaply. But the diamond you gave us is matchless, for it can lead everyone back to Godhead. We should not be so foolish as to miss this chance, but should serve you without considering the dictations of the mind. Everything you gave, Śrīla Prabhupāda, has immense value because of its relationship with the Supreme Lord.

Time is passing by, day after day, year after year. When will we become trustworthy instruments in your hands? When will we reject things and thoughts that are unfavorable to your service? When will our minds become submissive and always say yes to your instructions? Why do we waste so much time thinking “I am this body?”

You said, “Understand and accept the fact that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead.” This is the key formula for easily becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. You made everything so easy. Especially, you gave us for our association so many jewellike devotees who are working so hard to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. So many facilities—temples, books, and above all your crest jewel of mercy—help everyone understand and accept Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If one follows the process given by you, it is possible to develop nice Vaiṣṇava qualities, which are the source of shelter for everyone. Where are the doubts? No doubts. The goal of life is to surrender one hundred per-cent to you. Still, due to our low nature, we are still not convinced.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so glorious, so magnanimous, and so generous that you have accepted low rascals like us, who have hearts like black coal. Still you have faith in us. We regret very much having wasted so much valuable time taking great care of our bodily comforts, subtle and gross.

Now, on this most sacred anniversary of your divine advent, we pray to become honest beginners. We pray that with genuine enthusiasm we may sincerely try to begin to repay our huge debts to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You said, “ It is impossible to repay \[your debt to me\], but at least try to preach vigorously like me.” I see so many people who know nothing of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. There is no question of unemployment: simply throw all the *anarthas* in the fire of the *saṅkīrtana-yajña* and surrender everything completely. You are our worship able master and lord. Please allow us to remain in this wonderful ISKCON Society all our lives and serve you and the wonderful Vaiṣṇavas. By keeping this desire deep within our hearts, perhaps we will become sincere.

You came to this jungle-like material world and fearlessly preached like a lion, turning wild human beings into liberated souls. This is the successful result of your compassion upon the fallen souls.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the great mission of ISKCON you have started. We feel honored to be part of this movement, to be your servitors. We pray to receive a particle of your powerful mercy so that we may remain your eternal servants and have the courage to distribute your knowledge to the people.

All glories to your lotus feet!

(written by Durgama dāsa)

*Your servants in Padova, Italy,*

## Perth

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

All glories to Your Divine Grace, who are saving the world from the calamities of impersonalism and voidism by preaching pure *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti.* How and where do we begin to glorify your wonderful qualities? You are a Vaikuṇṭha man, and you have saved us. Our debt to you is eternal. We can never pay it back. However, by our practice of good *sādhana* and enthusiastic preaching, we can push forward your mission to establish a Golden Age in Kali-yuga, thus bringing satisfaction to Your Divine Grace.

With regard to Perth, in September 1972 you wrote the following letter to Upendra dāsa: “I am very glad to hear that you and your good wife went to Perth, Australia, for opening one ISKCON center. There is no doubt about it: I shall sanction your endeavor in this respect. I am always praying that my disciples will gradually increase our war against maya on all fronts…. So I expect the Perth center to be first class in every way.”

Well, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have our humble center for the battle against *maya.* Here’s how we are fighting : We have a restaurant, Gopal’s, distributing 600 plates of *prasādam* per week; three vegetarian clubs on campuses, distributing 250 plates per week; book distribution averaging around 760 books per week. Tens of thousands of West Australians now know you through book distribution. Our *hari-nāma* party goes out an average of twice a week. We have over a dozen devotees in the temple. Our Deities are the most munificent—Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitai. The Sunday feast is attended by 120 guests and congregation each week.

These figures show how we are just scratching the surface in our endeavors to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness and please Your Divine Grace. You have left us so much to do and given us the glory for preaching, but actually it is all happening by your arrangement.

We are indebted to you in every way for our very lives and souls. You are the *param-guru* of this great movement. Your fame is more widespread than that of any other great Vaiṣṇava *ācārya.* Such is your potency in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all the forgetful souls.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive us for all the offenses we may have knowingly or unknowingly committed in our humble attempts to serve your mission. Please empower us to tell others about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please empower us to establish a truly first-class center here in Perth, befitting Your Divine Grace.

*Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitai Mandir in Perth, Australia,*

## Puerto Rico

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you.

By materialistic calculation, Vrndāvana is located in a remote corner of the world. Yet from there, by the force of your intense desire, you started to transform our lives by beginning your translations and purports on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.*

Now your compassion has manifested before the eyes of the world. From the United States to Moscow, from Guyana to Fiji, from Puerto Rico to Africa, your immense concern for the welfare of all is bearing tangible fruit, and innumerable seeds are sprouting and, indeed, continue to be planted all across the earth. It doesn’t matter who they are or who we were—-prostitutes, meat-eaters, drug addicts, or madmen— all are watching their hearts be transformed by your mercy.

As your books are distributed and your instructions are followed, you continue to live and save condemned souls of this Kali-yuga. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please give us the strength to fulfill your desires and become your instruments in spreading the holy name.

*Your servants in Puerto Rico*

## Rome

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Despite the fearful influence of Kali-yuga, your transcendental mission is always fresh, bright, and enlivening for the fallen souls of this planet. Your presence is felt by all the devotees as so divine and so deep that it’s not possible to properly glorify you. Such an attempt is like an offering of lamps to the sun. What is the value?

Your importance in this world is comparable to Jesus Christ’s, and you can give salvation to whoever believes in your words. You left your inconceivable message for the next generations, and you taught us the wonderful facts about the Absolute Truth, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Please arrange for us to always have someone to associate with who has fully realized your teachings; in this way we will be able to maintain our purity and missionary spirit and help inspire others as well. If we cannot do this, our lives are virtually useless.

We pray that we can go on reading your books, serving your movement, and remembering your wonderful qualities eternally.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your granddisciples in Naples and Rome,*

## Romford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace’s appearance day.

In the marketplace of  
The holy name, anyone  
May purchase  
Pure love of God.  
The price?......faith.  
Faith…..that finally life’s  
Ultimate goal has been  
Irrefutably established.  
Faith…that a once-  
Cruel and miserly sinner  
Can be transformed  
Into an ecstatic pure associate  
Of the Lord.  
Faith……in the words of  
Kṛṣṇa’s servant,  
Unchanged and timeless.  
Faith….. which holds fast  
In the stormy ocean of Kali.  
Faith …..in the chanting of the holy  
Name as one’s only occupation.  
Faith ….that book distribution  
Is the only panacea  
For the pitiable condition  
Of modern man.  
Faith…..that your transcendental  
Body (ISKCON) is alive and well.  
Faith….that is allowed to grow,  
Fructify, and bear its long-  
Awaited fruit.  
But….we are faithless!  
How shall we then be saved?  
Here is an interesting  
Revelation:  
You, the chief salesman,  
Are distributing your wares,  
And for those who cannot afford them,  
You give them your goods as a gift.  
How wealthy you must be!  
Or….perhaps you are good friends  
With the store owner?  
Surely on one can  
Estimate the fortune of  
Anyone who meets this  
Amazing salesman!  
He appears to be so  
Magnanimous that soon  
The entire world will be given  
This priceless gift.  
Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please bestow your faith  
Upon us.

We beg to remain your eternal servants.

*The members of the Romford Nāma-hatta, U.K,*

## St. Louis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

Our greatest pleasure would be to offer the respect due to the dearmost servant of Lord Caitanya and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhantā Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. Genuine glorification comes in the form of service, forwards can be lip service only. Devotional service can be executed in different processes, but you gave book distribution the foremost importance as the greater *kīrtana.* You compared your BBT to your heart, and sending money to the BBT to pumping blood to your heart. When you heard that temples were collecting and spending all funds outside the BBT, you immediately sent a memorandum to all temple presidents saying that this must not continue! Then you gave the strict formula: twenty-five percent for temple maintenance and seventy-five per cent to the BBT! And you ended the letter by expressing your trust that we would all understand this.

In the Sixth Canto of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* Chapter 7, entitled ‘Indra offends Bṛhaspati, “you explain the importance of following the orders of the spiritual master. You say that it was because the demigods offended their spiritual master that they became powerless, and that it was because the demons worshiped their spiritual master properly that they developed sufficient strength to defeat even Lord Brahma. Although the demons were insignificant in comparison with him, they developed sufficient power to seize Brahmaloka! You quote a verse:

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**‘A lame man can cross mountains and a dumb man can become the greatest orator simply by pleasing the** ***guru.”***

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this day we pray that we may come to understand your teachings with much more depth. We do not claim to be very qualified than ourselves leave the shelter of the association of devotees and devotional service. To our amazement, somehow we are still under the shelter of your instructions! We know that our only qualification is to follow your instructions with sincerity. We have also made many mistakes, but just as the demons and the demigods both make mistakes in worshiping their *guru* but become properly situated simply by again offering the worship due the spiritual master, so we are also trying to rectify our mistakes and apply your teachings in the most Kṛṣṇa conscious way, and we are confident that this is pleasing to you.

We beg your mercy so that we can push on your mission with our full capacity. We hope to set an ideal example by staying engaged twenty-four hours in a day in your service. And despite any adverse circumstances or challenges, we hope to remain enthusiastic and fully determined not to compromise what you have worked so hard to establish. This is our success and happiness. As you said, the highest service is to go out and save others, but higher that that is to save ourselves!

You have written, “In a song we sign every day, Narottama dāsa Thakura says, *cakṣu-dāna dila yei, janme janme prabhu sei :* the *guru* gives spiritual insight to the disciple, and therefore the *guru* should be considered his master, life after life. Under no circumstance should the *guru* be disrespected” (*Bhag.* 6.7.21, purport).

*Yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo yasyaprasādān na gatiḥ kuto pi: “*By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement.”

May we always remain your humble servants!

*Your servants in ISKCON St. Louis,*

Govinda Datta dāsa / Cakrī dāsa / Divāṅga dāsa / Divyānanda dāsa / Sādhu-saṅga dāsa / Garga Muni dāsa / Gaura-Nitāi dāsa / Gaura-naṭarāja dāsa / Kaunteya dāsa / Śāntātmā-devī dāsī / Tulasī-devī dāsī / Śuka-sāgarī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Joena / Bhaktin Marilyn / Mrs. Kinnaird and congregation

## Saltillo

*Prabhupāda, tumi kṛpā-parāvara*  
*Jivera kārane āsiya prapañce*  
*līlā koile suvistāra*  
*Prabhupāda, niyate caraṇe-sthāna*  
*māga e paRāma, kāṇḍiya kāṇḍiya*  
*korohe karuṇā-dāna*

**O Prabhupāda! You are an ocean of mercy. You came from Goloka to this phenomenal world and manifested your divine pastimes to save the fallen souls.**  
**O Prabhupāda! This sinful soul laments again and again, begging you for an eternal place at your lotus feet. Please give me your mercy.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda:

Please accept our humble obeisances to your lotus feet.

Lord Gaurāṅga said this *sankiratan* movement would spread throughout the whole world. This is confirmed in the following verse from the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta (ādi* 7.26*):*

*Saj-jana, dur-jana, paṅgu, jaḍa, andha-gaṇa*  
*Prema-vanyāya ḍuvaila jagatera jana*

**“The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will inundate the entire world and drown everyone, whether one be a gentleman, a rogue, or even lame, invalid, or blind.”**

Now this prediction is becoming a fact by your mercy. You are the personality who embodies the truth of this prediction; therefore you said, “Preach, preach, preach,” “Distribute books, distribute books, distribute books,” “Book distribution is my only hope,” “Book distribution is my life.” We are very unintelligent; by ourselves we know nothing. But these instructions of yours have descended in the *paramparā* through a divine, unbroken channel of pure devotees, and thus preaching is also our mission. We take shelter of your sacred order.

*Your servants at the Rādhā-Govinda mandīra in Saltillo, Mexico,*

Hare Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Gaurāṅga -śakti dāsa / Madhusūdana-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Maria Elizabeth / Bhaktin Elbira

**Congregation**

Satya Narayana Kurvadi / Kanta Kurvadi

## San Diego

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Of all the appearance days we celebrate throughout the year, Vyāsa-pūjā is called the most blessed, because on this day we honor the great soul who made the other days count for us. Lord Kṛṣṇa has come many times to save the world, but on this day in 1896, Śrīla Prabhupāda came to give Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy to those who could not accept it otherwise.

The world has been saved many times, but each of us has been saved just once. If Lord Kṛṣṇa came a million times a day, we still could not take one drop of His mercy without the devotional seed that Śrīla Prabhupāda planted in the hearts of his disciples.

Whether we are Śrīla Prabhupāda’s disciples, who received the seed directly from him, or his granddisciples, who received the seed from his disciples, Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy came to us on a summer day on 1965 when Śrīla Prabhupāda got off the *Jaladuta* in New York.

He had almost no money, just a few personal belongings and some books, but he was more powerful than any king, and his books held the first Bhaktivedanta purports. He could do what no one else in the world could do: he could plant the seed of devotion in the hearts of the *mlecchas* and *yavanas.*

Lord Kṛṣṇa came five thousand years ago to save the lost souls of this world, but where were we? Lost somewhere, no doubt, but too sinful to take advantage of the blessing. Lord Caitanya came only five hundred years ago, and we managed to get away at that time too. But skilful as we were in fleeing the mercy of the Lord, we were caught by the Bhaktivedanta purports.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñanāñjana-dśalākayā*  
*cahṣur unmilitam yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Kṛṣṇa, the expert electrician, can transform hot into cold, material into spiritual. Śrīla Prabhupāda, his pure devotee, transformed an ordinary typewriter into the touch of knowledge.

Lord Kṛṣṇa left a storehouse of love of God, but He left it locked. Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda broke the lock and distributed the content to everyone, qualified or unqualified, but we didn’t want any. We couldn’t tell what it was. Then Prabhupāda came and opened our eyes with the Bhaktivedanta purports.

We celebrate the appearance days of the Lord and remember His compassion on the fallen souls rotting in this material world. On Vyāsa-pūjā day we remember the one whose compassion alone brought us the compassion of the Lord. *Yasya prasādād bhagavat prasāsdo yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*:” By the mercy of the spiritual master, one receives the mercy of the Lord. Without the mercy of the spiritual master, no one can make any advancement.”

Lord Kṛṣṇa confirms it:” My dear Pārtha, “He tells Arjuna, “one who claims to be My devotee is not so. Only a person who is the devotee of My devotee is actually My devotee.”

*Hā hā prabhu koro doyā, deho more pada-chayā: “*O spiritual master, be merciful and give me the shade of your lotus feet.” Śrīla Prabhupāda gave us the seed, he gave us the water of the *mahā-mantra,* and he gave us the shade of his lotus feet, the only place where this plant will grow. Lord Kṛṣṇa gave us Śrīla Prabhupāda and Vyāsa-pūjā day, but Śrīla Prabhupāda had to tell us.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in San Diego, California,*

## San Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this auspicious day, on which we are celebrating your appearance in this mortal world.

*Yat-pāda-saṁśrayāh sūta munayaḥ praśamāyanāḥ*  
*Sadyaḥ punanty upaspṛṣṭāḥ svadhuny-āpo ‘nusevayā*

**“O Suta, those great sages who have completely taken shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord can at once sanctify those who come in touch with them, whereas the waters of the Ganges can sanctify only after prolonged use.”**

This time of year, when we write your Vyāsa-pūjā offering, our hearts are filled with love for you because it reminds us that we no longer have the supporting strength of your physical strength of your physical presence, and that our association with you is now manifest through your great *saṅkīrtana* movement.

We wish that we could turn to you with our lamentations and anxieties during this great period of oppression we are presently suffering, whether due to *karma* or Lord Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, in our unending struggle with the materialists. Nevertheless, we are gradually becoming enthused because we know that by your touch (divine cause) we will someday realize the end of the worst of wars, the war within our lusty minds and corrupted hearts.

What can a helpless child ask of his merciful father? By your grace, O divine preceptor, we ask you only to keep us bound to your devotional service. This will be possible only if you allow us to remain in this great ISKCON movement, which is the ”daily bread” that will give us the spiritual strength to gradually attain the nectar for which we are always anxious—*prema bhakti.*

This is the beloved day on which we all worship you as a divine gift from the Supreme Lord. Lord Kṛṣṇa has been very kind upon us, for He has sent you as the personification of His mercy. This auspicious day should be the foremost of festival days on our Vaiṣṇava calendar because if you had not given us Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we would have never celebrated any Vaiṣṇava festivals.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to be sanctified at once by the mercy of your lotus feet.

*Your fallen servants in San Salvador, El Salvador.*

## Santos

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to the greatest well-wisher of mankind the world has ever seen. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who never stepped back before the wall of *māyā.* Like a hurricane, you demolished all the fortress walls of the materialists, triumphantly establishing the *saṅkīrtana* movement with its many *āsrmas.* These are safe shelters for all who believe in you as the savior who can end the suffering of our present life and bestow eternal bliss in our future life.

All glories to the most tolerant and merciful personality the world has ever seen! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who have everlasting patience and kindness. You showed us how to eat, sleep, procreate, and even defend ourselves as true human beings.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who in each and every gesture showed us the importance of surrendering to God, who with each word purified us with the chanting of the names, qualities, and pastimes of the Lord, who with each book instructed us with philosophical, practical, profound lessons concerning our duties in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

All glories to the *guru of gurus,* the master of masters, the dearmost intimate servant of Śrīmati Rādhārani. With absolute certainty you are very dear to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa; otherwise you could not have flooded the world with the most elevated love for God in Lord Caitanya’s mood.

What else can I say? Anything I try to say about Your Divine Grace will always be insignificant before your magnificence. I feel that even more important than chanting your glories, which I am not qualified to do, is promising within my heart, with all possible honestly, that I will never cheat you, that I will never stop trying to serve you with all my strength despite so many limitations, and that I will never stop paying obeisances to you by rendering practical service to push on your divine mission of saving the world. I hope I can always envision your smiling face in my mind and heart.

*Your useless servants in Santos, Brazil.*

## Scotland

We offer our respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedānta Swami Prabhupāda, who is so dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.

Who, desiring deliverance from the terrible vices of this age of iron and quarrel, is unwilling to take shelter at your lotus feet?

You are the shelter for all fallen souls of this very dark and very dangerous age, and through your purports all fallen souls will be redirected back to home, back to Godhead.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you and your ISKCON movement.

We, the aspiring devotees of your Scottish *yātrā,* wish to thank you for mercifully engaging us so very nicely in your service. Such an opportunity is so very rare—-it is by far the most precious gift one can ever be offered.

By your causeless mercy we are engaged in your book distribution program, and we find that we are becoming more and more anxious and enthused about distributing more and more of your soul-saving books. We are finding great life in your book distribution, and are discovering that nothing pleases you more than to hear about the countless thousands and thousands of your books are being distributed throughout this planet.

As time passes, we are appreciating more and more that simply by the distribution of your books, absolutely everything will come about in due course of time, just as you told us. We are becoming more and more enthusiastic in our general service. The people that we are meeting on the streets and in the businesses are showing more interest; the more books they take, the friendlier they become. In fact, they are now prepared to help in many other ways. They are generously supplying *bhoga,* toiletries, paper for printing, clothing… Practically all the needs of the temple and the devotees are supplied by the people we meet through book distribution. You tried to convince us so many times that everything will be taken care of if we simply distribute your books. Now we are fully experiencing the absolute truth of your instructions. We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being so kind and giving us this practical realization, and also for constantly providing for us so very nicely. We pray that we may never at any time become wasteful in any way.

We would like to carry on distributing your books more and more, until every home in Scotland has at least one of your publications. We beg for your mercy and permission to attempt to realize this goal within one or two years. Please give us your blessings.

Being worthless, we do not really have much of anything to offer you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; in fact, whatever worth there is in us was given by you alone. So all we can really do is offer ourselves in your service continually, hoping that out of your causeless mercy you may find some little use for us fallen, insignificant souls.

You have given us everything—the holy name, shelter at your lotus feet, your temples, the Deity form of the Lord to serve and worship, the purports in your books to guide us and direct us all the way home, service to you in so many different ways, the process for bathing the body, the mind, and the heart….everything we know and have is coming directly from you You have given life to us otherwise spiritually dead *jīvas.* So, since our lives have been saved by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are solely your property. Please help us do as you desire.

Only by our serving your lotus feet can we achieve full *prema-bhakti.* Only from your lotus mouth does *divya-jñāna* emanate. Only by your book distribution will the fallen souls of this world be saved. Only your ISKCON will give full shelter to the fallen soul of this perilous age. Only by your causeless mercy will we be able to please the Vaiṣṇavas and Kṛṣṇa.

So on this day, as on every day, we can offer you only what you have given us, with the promise that we will try our best to distribute your mercy.

Now, feeling somewhat useless yet inspired, we beg to go on doing what you would have us do.

Please forgive this very meager attempt at glorifying you and the service you have performed for the fallen souls of this age.

Begging for the ability to fully accept your guidance and teachings we wish to remain situated in your service continually.

*Your insignificant servants of the Scottish Yātrā,*

## South London

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to your divine Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Although we have no qualifications to glorify you or your transcendental activities on this most auspicious day of your appearance, if you so desire, please accept this weak attempt.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy you descended to this planet just to save the foolish conditioned souls who are so entangled in this material world. At an advanced age you came to the West to establish a worldwide movement to preach the *saṅkīrtana-yajna* of this age and fulfill the promise of Lord Caitanya that the holy name will be chanted in every town and village. Through your sublime classes and transcendental Bhaktivedanta purports in those so many wonderful books, you are constantly and uncompromisingly instructing and benefiting the whole world.

Sirla Prabhupāda, in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.3.43), Sūta Gosvāmī says to the great sages in the forest of Naimiṣāraṇya:

*Kṛṣṇa sva-dhāmopagate dharma-jñānādibhiḥ saha*  
*Kalau naṣṭa-dṛśām eṣa purāṇārko ‘dhunoditaḥ*

**“This** ***Bhāgavata Purana*** **is as brilliant as the sun, and it has arisen just after the departure of Lord Kṛṣṇa to His own abode, accompanied by religion, knowledge, etc. Persons who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in the age of Kali shall get light from this** ***Purāṇa.”***

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so good as the book *Bhāgavata* because you showed by your perfect example how a devotee not only preaches from the book *Bhāgavata,* and in that way his preaching becomes effective because “purity is the force.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are the foolish conditioned souls who have been misled by the illusory energy, but somehow, by Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s transcendental mercy, we have come into contact with your movement and are trying to sincerely serve you by serving the devotees who are completely engaged in your service. However, the debt that we owe you can never be repaid, and we only hope that you will accept the insignificant service that we clumsily offer.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us, who are faithless and full of *anarthas,* become strong and fearless in the execution of devotional service under the guidance of your pure devotees. If you so desire, please enable us to glorify you by spreading the transcendental knowledge of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, which you have kindly brought to the West.

*Your insignificant granddisciples at the South London yātrā,*

## South Trinidad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this most auspicious occasion of your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thakura writes that days such as Ekādaśī and Janmāṣṭamī are the mother of devotion, the *bhakti-latā-bīja,* would never have been planted in our hearts!

The *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* states that by serving those devotees who are completely free of vice, one gains affinity for hearing the messages of Vāsudeva. Out of great compassion, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have afforded us the only means by which we may be attracted to hearing about Kṛṣṇa. And only in this way can we purify our hearts of all material desires and attain *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* thus becoming freed from our endless sufferings in this miserable material world.

The pure devotee turns all places into places of pilgrimage due to his carrying the Personality of Godhead within his heart, and thus we see so many holy *dhāmas,* your ISKCON temples, being created in the most sinful environments. You freely give us your holy association, and so even in places that you never visited, like Trinidad, there are now such places of pilgrimage.

Through these *tīrthas* and through your books, your personal instructions, and your faithful followers who continue spreading your teachings, we are given that invaluable opportunity to serve the pure devotee.

Although the debt to the spiritual master can never be repaid, we submit this humble prayer: that we may always endeavor to please you by engaging in the *saṅkīrtana* mission as taught by you. We are incompetent and full of faults, but we pray for the determination to more enthusiastically preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in our *prabhu-datta-deśa* and thus extend the mercy you are giving us to countless other suffering living entities.

*Your insignificant servants in Trinidad.*

## Stockholm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You appeared suddenly in the midst of confusion, entering the hearts of thousands of bewildered souls, subduing their false egos, enchanting their minds, embarrassing their so-called intelligence, driving out their sinful habits and esoteric speculative views, drowning them in devotional service to the Lord.

Your words, simple and clear but deep as thunder, woke up the pious and impious from their sleep. Brushing aside mundane liberalism, you settled down in the heats of your devoted followers as an absolute authority, crashing down their youthful lust, closing their mouths in the midst of their arrogant speeches, and making their tongues resound with only “Kṛṣṇa.” Drowning everybody in the sound of the holy name, you made it clear that devotional service is not some esoteric mundane concept but rather a living reality full of activity, full of realized knowledge, and full of bliss. You raised the university speculators from their chairs and brought them into the busy streets of the Kali-yuga cities, dancing in *harināma* and distributing your books. You made all these miserable people into radiant devotees of the Lord, spreading the message of Lord Caitanya all over the world. Even the most stone-hearted, stubborn impersonalist found himself embracing the surprised conditioned souls by delivering to them *Bhagavad-gīta, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, and Caitanya-caritāmṛta.* Too slow is the mind to understand what happened. The sort of happiness you gave us is beyond the cloudy materialistic mind and the mundane senses as well. Just by the touch of your books, even for a few seconds, the spiritual life of a deathlike ignorant being starts.

As Kali-yuga goes on, every day we discover another precious gift you gave us—- another valuable instruction you left behind, like a safety belt, preventing us from drowned in the ocean of *maya.*

Thank you once again and again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for these wonderful instructions. We beg you for the power to preserve and worship them as they are, unchanged and pure. Please don’t let the poison of speculation deteriorate your simple and sublime words and cause the clouds of forgetfulness to cover our minds. Please don’t let our service to the Lord become merely so-called sophisticated speech. Please give us the strength to break through the silence of our minds and freely call out for your mercy. Refreshed, we will then dive into the cities of sin, looking for new-*bhakta* candidates, for new Jāgais and Mādhāis, as you did; leaving behind your transcendental literature, as you did; establishing spiritual fortresses, as you did; pushing back the insanity spread in the name of so-called independence and freedom, as you did.

Your books are like time bombs, ready to detonate any mind and destroy the ironlike coverings of people’s hearts. Just as you moved through the jungles of monstrous cities, bringing the dead to life again, so let us follow in your footsteps, feeding the hungry with *prasādam* and eternal bliss. What a life you gave us, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Full of meaning and satisfaction.

How steadily and patiently you are taking care of us still, despite all the setbacks! How tirelessly you worked day and night to make others happy! Yow wrote books like the most wonderful poet, you took care of your daily affairs of your society like the most expert manager, you were gently, renounced, and pure like the most humble sage, you challenged the most established doctrines of materialism and impersonalism like a ferocious transcendental warrior, you made your disciples exclaim in amazement: "Prabhupāda who are you?”

Anyone who even begins to understand only a tiny fraction of your transcendental opulences will simply bow down at your lotus feet, stunned and eternally obliged.

Whatever may be the lesson you are teaching, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us never stop learning it. There is so much to know. If we can reflect merely a spark of the depth and gravity of your words, people may be able to understand what wonderful gifts you brought us. Book after book, one preaching program after another, one Sunday feast after another, one *prasādam* plate after another, let us keep on conspiring after *maya,* turning more and more souls towards your *vyāsāsana,* surrendering their lives to Your Divine Grace.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the savior of the most fallen!

*Your servants at the Hare Kṛṣṇa Center in Stockholm, Sweden,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Maṇidhara dāsa

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Switzerland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

In the *Bhāgavatam* class, we are now reading the Twenty-sixth Chapter of the Fourth Canto. This chapter is actually very powerful, for is shows us the living entity’s entanglement in material sense gratification—although without your purports this chapter would have no meaning for us, or it would sound like an ordinary love story. But through your purports everything becomes understandable and transcendental. Through your transcendental vision and intelligence each word of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* comes alive. It is described that the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is very sweet and nectarean, but by the touch of Śukadeva Gosvāmī it became even sweeter. And now through the mercy of the whole *guru-paramparā* and Your Divine Grace, it has become sweeter still.

In your purports there are no theories, speculations, or illusion. Your purports are so powerful that they first of all expose all our material desires and attachments, and then just by reciting and hearing these books regularly one becomes more and more purified.

Sometimes the purification is very painful due to our hoglike mentality, and thus the mind thinks," Oh, this is overemphasized or this is too heavy.” But as Kali-yuga progresses and people become more and more degraded, one realized that the *Bhāgavatam* is right and that the purports of the *ācāryas* are right.

Another wonder of your books is that no matter which book we read, we always think, “This is the best one!” Thus we realize that actually each of your books is the best. As you wrote in one of your letters (Jan. 5, 1973), “Actually the whole thing rests upon these books.” And May 4, 1976: “It is a transcendental literature. Nobody can challenge it. It is done so nicely, without any spot, the spotless Purana.” We are very proud to have these nice books, and to have you and your mercy through your eternal instructions. By reading and distributing your books we will always feel fresh because the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and your purports are always fresh. By your mercy we can always go on with the distribution of the always-fresh message.

Concerning service to pure devotees, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thakura writes in one of his purports to Śrī Mahaḥ-Śikṣā: ”The Supreme Lord’s beloved devotees are by nature unalloyed servitors, and their spiritual status is incomparable. They are the generals in Kṛṣṇa’s army of devotees, and divine rays from the Lord’s pleasure-giving potency, or *hlādinī-śakti,* illuminate their hearts. This potency, is moreover, is easily transferred to other hearts, where it vanquishes all contaminations and generates transcendental love of Godhead. The sincere Vaiṣṇava devotees embrace the dust of these unalloyed servitors’ lotus feet, their food remnants, and their sacred instructions, for these all have the transcendental potency to impregnate one’s heart with the divine *hlādinī-śakti.”*

The import of the sentence “This potency, moreover, is easily transferred to other hearts, where it vanquishes all contaminations and generates transcendental love for Godhead” can be realized everyday as we see how people become more and more interested in your books, and how they always buy more. At the places where we go the most, the people become more and more eager for these books. They become more and more trained up by your *saṅkīrtana* soldiers. In this way the innocent people become used to us, and the demons become practically destroyed. Many years ago they always barked at us, but nowadays the people appreciate or at least tolerate the devotees with the books.

One day, while I was distributing your books, a man and a lady from an insurance company who had made some propaganda against us approached me and asked what I was selling. Initially, I was a little skeptical about their motives, and therefore I told them that these books were highly religious, something for pious people, and that I did not think they would like them. Then they said: "But this is exactly the kind of book we’re interested in!” Then I said:” You’re really sure? They are all about God and devotional service. You really want such books?” Then I gave each of them a book, and in the end they happily walked away in the possession of six of your books.

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray for the dust of your lotus feet, which will enable us to always be engaged in your transcendental mission. Thus by your mercy everybody will get at least one of your books. And we know that you will not be satisfied until everybody on this planet has at least one of your books.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s transcendental book distribution *ki jaya!*

(written by Rohiṇī-suta dāsa)

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You crossed the ocean on the order of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhantā Sarasvatī Thākura, to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the Westerners. That was the desire of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhantā, and you fulfilled that desire for him and the whole disciplic succession. You are therefore the greatest disciple of your spiritual master and are extraordinarily empowered by him.

We can see practically what you accomplished: a worldwide society of Vaiṣṇavas and a legacy of literature within which rests the plan for the deliverance and upliftment of the whole of the humanity.

The whole of ISKCON goes on only because of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. The spreading of Krsna consciousness is under your control. You have the mandate, and only one who accepts you can preach the message of Lord Caitanyadeva. Others may try, but you are the Jagat-guru, the *ācārya* for this age. Others will come in the future, but they also cannot preach without understanding your position as the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON.

The Gaudiya Math was destroyed because they disobeyed the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhantā Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. Instead of forming a Governing Body Commission and co-operating together to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness, they fell victim to *maya* in the form of pride and ambition:” I will be *guru,” “*I am the *ācārya,”* etc.

ISKCON is your legacy for the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda. ISKCON is in fact nondifferent from you, being the embodiment of your desires and those of Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda.

Understanding your position within ISKCON is the solution to all our *guru-*related problems. We members of ISKCON have to take more shelter of you and your plans; then we will surmount all our difficulties. If we are unsure of our desires, we can read your books more. But even then our conclusions must be in line with the deliberations of the GBC body, which is your representative.

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we in Sydney, Australia, thank you for your unlimited mercy upon us. We don’t ask you for mercy because we know that you are always giving mercy. This we can see by the pain and pleasure within ISKCON’s growth.

Also, we would like to thank you for all the mercy you gave us on your visits to Australia and beg that you will continue to engage us as your servants, although we are surely very unqualified.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants in Sydney, Australia.*

## Tampa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

We, your humble disciples in Tampa, Florida, are making a sincere attempt to carry out your divine instructions, to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the poor, suffering conditioned souls. You have kindly opened our eyes wide and revealed the Absolute Truth to us. For this we are forever indebted.

By your purity as a bona fide spiritual master, we are receiving strength to execute your order.

Please bestow your blessings upon us so we may not deviate from your guidance.

Glory and bliss  
On this wonderful day,  
When heaven meets earth,  
From Viṣṇu a ray.  
As a child in your stroller,  
Before the Deities you would stay,  
Appearing among pure devotees,  
Like your father, Gour Mohan De.  
As a child serving the Lord  
By pulling His Rath-cart,  
You led the kīrtana  
For the Lord of the heart.  
Then at six you wanted Deities  
To worship with great art,  
Manifesting pure devotion,  
From the Lord you wouldn’t part.  
In college you learned what’s useful  
To serve Kṛṣṇa as a tool,  
And how to reject the rubbish,  
Which is meant for the fool.  
Your life is a beacon,  
Following Kṛṣṇa’s rule.  
Śrīla Bhaktisiddhantā,  
Your guru, you met.  
“Preach in the West,” his order  
in your heart he set.  
Fulfilling his order,  
You went off to the West,  
Doing or dying,  
You did your best.  
You wonderfully brought Kṛṣṇa  
And picked us up too,  
Exemplifying the best disciple,  
Living as the perfect guru.

*Your humble servants in Tampa, Florida,*

Vivasvvān dāsa / Keśavānanda dāsa / Madana-mohana dāsa / Pañcalī-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-pritā-devī dāsī / Meena and Keri Avatara

## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Ever since I can remember, I’d always daydreamed about what it would like to be around during an important time in history. I’d see a movie or read a book about the life of Christ or someone else important, and I’d think how foolish those people were back then who didn’t grasp the significance of that moment. They couldn’t see the greatness of Christ.

But not me. If only I’d been there I would have understood the historical significance of that time, the greatness of that person’s teachings, and I would have sacrificed everything. I wouldn’t let my petty desires and emotions cloud my vision. No, not me. Surely I would have had the intelligence and sense of timing to see.

I always thought what a curse it would be to get stuck in one of those blank moments in history when nothing but the hard struggle occupied man’s time or thoughts. Oh, to have the proper sense of timing and be born when history was to be made! When all the mysteries that haunt humanity were solved and the real meaning of life was finally understood! Oh, to live in such a time!

But, my dear Godbrothers and Godsisters, such a time is now!

When future generations look back at this moment in time from a distance of one hundred, five hundred, or even a thousand years from now, they will lament that they were not there at the beginning of the golden age, when Prabhupāda was present. Just as we look back and wish we could have been around at the time Lord Caitanya was present or know His followers just after He left the planet, in the future they’ll think it a great privilege for someone to have been a disciple of Sirla Prabhupāda or even to have known one.

And what a unique privilege it is to be Śrīla Prabhupāda’s disciple! People are judging Śrīla Prabhupāda by what we, his disciples, do in his physical absence. Recently a prominent religion professor who had met Śrīla Prabhupāda on several occasions and had great respect for him remarked that it’s just too bad that Prabhupāda didn’t have the foresight to see how his disciples would act after his departure; otherwise he might have done differently. There is, of course, nothing wrong with Prabhupāda’s vision. It is our lack of vision to see the significance of this moment in history and the greatness of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s work and teachings.

In the *Bhagavad-gīta* Kṛṣṇa says that the living entity is unchangeable, but there is one thing about the living entity that does change, and that is his consciousness. It can change from material consciousness to spiritual consciousness, from forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa to remembrance. My dear Prabhus, let us not forget what Prabhupāda gave us, what he saved us from. Let us be conscious of this time in history and the immensely important role we play.

Of course mistakes have been made, and there are plenty of reasons for bitterness and a lack of trust. But these mistakes were not made by Prabhupāda, and what he has given still remains. Has not the Lord invested all His transcendental potencies in His holy names, or are they meaningless words? Are Deities the Supreme Personality of Godhead, or are They just wood and stone? Are Prabhupāda’s books transcendental vibrations, the lawbooks for the next ten thousand years, or just paper and ink? Is *prasādam* spiritual food, or just vegetarian preparations? Are we all going to die and be responsible for our acts, or was it all just a lie? These are the gifts of Śrīla Prabhupāda, and they are as available and potent as they ever were. The only thing that has changed is our attitude. Śrīla Prabhupāda says in *The Nectar of Instruction* that advancement is based on the practitioner’s attitude. Let’s have the right attitude toward these gifts and toward the time in history; then we will work hard and put Śrīla Prabhupāda in his proper place in history.

*From Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants at ISKCON Toronto (New Ramuṇa Dhāma),*

Bhakti-mārga Swami / Rādhā-Mādhava dāsa / Hara-kunāra dāsa / Dharma-prāṇa dāsa / Satyavrata dāsa / Gokularañjana dāsa / Acārya dāsa / Brahmā dāsa / Vāmanadeva dāsa / Janardana dāsa / Śyamsundara dāsa / Bhakti-yoga dāsa / Dīna Caitanya dāsa / Jagat-pati dāsa / Nara-Nārāyaṇa dāsa / Sattva-tanu-devī dāsī / Bhakti-bhāva-devī dāsī / Bhakta Lajos / Bhakta Richard / Bhakta Narayan / Bhakta Kali / Bhakta Jacqueline

## Udhampur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotuslike feet, the shelter of all Vaiṣṇavas. We feel incompetent to glorify a transcendental personality like yourself, but we pray that since you are a true representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa, you shall accept our humble offering on your appearance day.

It was September 1987 that one of your dear sons, His Holiness Nava-yogendra Maharājā, came to this part of the Indian subcontinent to fulfill your mission of establishing the Śrīla Prabhupāda Ashram. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have experienced miracles since then. We also saw many ups and downs, but Your Divine Grace came to our rescue every time, guiding us through your books and personal instructions, teaching us how a Vaiṣṇava should face everything for Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You are personally present here in this *āśrāma:* many devotees have experienced your presence in dreams, seeing you sitting on the *vvyāsāsana* talking to devotees or sometimes walking in the temple. Whenever we celebrate Śrī Rāma-navami or Śrī Janmāṣṭamī, you make it a big success, ignoring our insignificance and careless nature. You are so merciful that even though we constantly commit offenses, you always forgive us.

You suffered so much to make the holy name of Kṛṣṇa available to every living entity on this planet. We are indebted to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, life after life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray at the dust of your lotus feet: please bless us with your mercy so that we may attain some position as the servants of the servant of your servant. Please give us the intelligence to nicely establish Śrīla Prabhupāda Ashram so that it can be a shelter for every sincere seeker of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept once more our most fallen obeisances at the dust of your lotuslike feet and bless us with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

*Your eternal servants in Udhampur, India,*

## Vancouver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You gave us a vision, a path to follow. You set down the guidelines so we would not be led astray. The path of perfection. A way of life. Therefore, let us this day offer our combined respects and humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Although before us we find all the necessary means to attain the supreme goal, still a confusion separates us from the truth. Just as a spoon enters food but does not taste it, so we, due to our lack of purity, fail to properly assimilate your transcendental instructions. We feel lost.

When will we cleanse the dust from the mirror of our hearts so we will be able to understand your message? How will we be able to help others if we ourselves remain covered?

Still, we feel hope and thus carry on, encouraged by your words in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* where you say, “Transcendental loving service is never to be compared to the forced service of the material world. There \[in the spiritual world\] the transcendental loving service is due to spontaneous love. A reflected glimpse of such service is experienced in the service of the mother unto the son, a friend’s service unto the friend, or the wife’s service unto the husband. These reflections of service by friends, and wives are not forced, but are due to love only.”

We pray to be able to develop this loving mood here in New Gokula Dhāma. We feel encouraged to hear you say, “ When something is arranged by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, one should not be disturbed by it, even if it appears to be a reverse according to one’s calculations. For example: sometimes we see that a powerful preacher is killed, or sometimes he is put into difficulty, just as Haridāsa Ṭhākura was. He was a great devotee who came into this material world to execute the will of the Lord by preaching the Lord’s glories. But Haridāsa was punished at the hands of the Kazi by being beaten in twenty-two market places. Similarly, Lord Jesus Christ was crucified and Prahlāda Mahāraja was put through so many tribulations. The Pāṇḍavas, who were direct friends of Kṛṣṇa, lost their kingdom, their wife was insulted, and they had to undergo many severe tribulations. Seeing all these reverses affect devotees, one should not be disturbed; one should simply understand that in these matters there must be some plan of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The *Bhāgavatam’s* conclusion is that a devotee is never disturbed by such reverses. He accepts even reverse conditions as the grace of the Lord. One who continues to serve the Lord even in reverse conditions is assured that he will go back to Godhead, back to the Vaikuntha planets.” *(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.16.37 purport)

We pray to live simply and honestly, realizing that advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness does not depend on any materially prosperous or distressed condition.

We pray for pure vision to situate ourselves properly in Kṛṣṇa’s social structure as our community develops.

On this year’s Vyāsa-pūjā day, we wish to encourage all your disciples to remember that there is only one goal: to serve Kṛṣṇa to one’s best ability.

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, let us remember and put into actual practice this advice of yours to us: “Disunity between individual souls is so strong within this material world that even in a society of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, members sometimes appear disunited due to having different opinions and leaning toward material things. Actually, in Kṛṣṇa consciousness there cannot be two opinions. If there is some disagreement over service, such disagreement is to be taken as spiritual. Those who are actually engaged in the service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead cannot be disunited in any circumstance.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 4.30.8 purport)

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we stand helplessly before you, begging for your mercy, praying for pure vision to be able to cooperate together.

*Your servants at New Gokula Dhāma, home of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana (Vancouver, Canada),*
